### [1] [LLMORPH: Automated Metamorphic Testing of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23611)
*Steven Cho,Stefano Ruberto,Valerio Terragni*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LLMORPH是一个基于蜕变测试的自动化测试工具，专门用于评估LLM在NLP任务中的可靠性，无需人工标注数据即可发现模型不一致行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型测试面临的主要挑战是缺乏自动化oracle来验证输出正确性，且人工标注数据成本高昂。需要一种无需标注数据的自动化测试方法来评估LLM的可靠性。

Method: LLMORPH采用蜕变测试方法，利用蜕变关系从源测试输入生成后续输入，通过检测模型输出间的不一致性来发现故障行为。工具设计支持扩展到任意LLM、NLP任务和蜕变关系集。

Result: 在评估中，对GPT-4、LLAMA3和HERMES 2三个先进LLM应用了36个蜕变关系，覆盖四个NLP基准测试，执行了超过561,000次测试，成功自动暴露了模型的不一致性。

Conclusion: LLMORPH为研究人员和开发者提供了一种有效的自动化测试工具，能够在不依赖人工标注数据的情况下评估LLM在NLP任务中的鲁棒性，并展示了良好的可扩展性和实用性。

Abstract: Automated testing is essential for evaluating and improving the reliability of Large Language Models (LLMs), yet the lack of automated oracles for verifying output correctness remains a key challenge. We present LLMORPH, an automated testing tool specifically designed for LLMs performing NLP tasks, which leverages Metamorphic Testing (MT) to uncover faulty behaviors without relying on human-labeled data. MT uses Metamorphic Relations (MRs) to generate follow-up inputs from source test input, enabling detection of inconsistencies in model outputs without the need of expensive labelled data. LLMORPH is aimed at researchers and developers who want to evaluate the robustness of LLM-based NLP systems. In this paper, we detail the design, implementation, and practical usage of LLMORPH, demonstrating how it can be easily extended to any LLM, NLP task, and set of MRs. In our evaluation, we applied 36 MRs across four NLP benchmarks, testing three state-of-the-art LLMs: GPT-4, LLAMA3, and HERMES 2. This produced over 561,000 test executions. Results demonstrate LLMORPH's effectiveness in automatically exposing inconsistencies.

</details>


### [2] [LLMLOOP: Improving LLM-Generated Code and Tests through Automated Iterative Feedback Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23613)
*Ravin Ravi,Dylan Bradshaw,Stefano Ruberto,Gunel Jahangirova,Valerio Terragni*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LLMLOOP：一个自动化精炼LLM生成源代码和测试用例的框架，通过五个迭代循环解决编译错误、静态分析问题、测试失败等问题，生成高质量代码和测试用例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在生成源代码方面表现出色，但生成的代码常存在编译错误或逻辑错误。研究人员和开发者在检查和精炼LLM生成的代码时经常重复劳动，浪费精力。需要自动化工具来高效改进LLM生成的代码和测试用例。

Method: 提出LLMLOOP框架，采用五个迭代循环：1) 解决编译错误；2) 处理静态分析问题；3) 修复测试用例失败；4) 通过变异分析提高测试质量；5) 确保生成高质量测试用例，既作为验证机制也作为回归测试套件。

Result: 在HUMANEVAL-X基准测试上评估LLMLOOP，结果显示该工具能有效精炼LLM生成的输出，提高代码和测试用例的质量。

Conclusion: LLMLOOP框架通过自动化迭代精炼过程，显著提高了LLM生成代码和测试用例的质量，减少了人工检查和修复的工作量，为LLM代码生成提供了有效的质量保证机制。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are showing remarkable performance in generating source code, yet the generated code often has issues like compilation errors or incorrect code. Researchers and developers often face wasted effort in implementing checks and refining LLM-generated code, frequently duplicating their efforts. This paper presents LLMLOOP, a framework that automates the refinement of both source code and test cases produced by LLMs. LLMLOOP employs five iterative loops: resolving compilation errors, addressing static analysis issues, fixing test case failures, and improving test quality through mutation analysis. These loops ensure the generation of high-quality test cases that serve as both a validation mechanism and a regression test suite for the generated code. We evaluated LLMLOOP on HUMANEVAL-X, a recent benchmark of programming tasks. Results demonstrate the tool's effectiveness in refining LLM-generated outputs.

</details>


### [3] [Detect--Repair--Verify for LLM-Generated Code: A Multi-Language, Multi-Granularity Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23633)
*Cheng Cheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个检测-修复-验证（DRV）工作流来评估LLM生成软件的安全漏洞管理，构建了EduCollab基准测试，发现迭代DRV能提高安全正确率，但效果受修复范围影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏对LLM生成软件安全性的端到端评估，存在四个研究空白：缺少基于测试的基准、管道级有效性证据有限、检测报告作为修复指导的可靠性不明确、以及验证下修复的可信度不确定。

Method: 构建EduCollab多语言基准测试（PHP、JavaScript、Python），包含可执行的功能和漏洞测试套件。在项目、需求和文件三个粒度上比较未修复基线、单次检测-修复和有限迭代DRV工作流，测量安全正确产出率，分析中间产物和迭代轨迹。

Result: 有限迭代DRV相比单次修复能提高安全正确产出率，但在项目级别效果不均，在更窄的修复范围内效果更明显。检测报告对下游修复通常有用但可靠性不一致，修复可信度强烈依赖修复范围。

Conclusion: 研究强调了基于测试的端到端评估LLM漏洞管理工作流的必要性，检测报告的可靠性和修复可信度需要进一步改进，修复范围是影响效果的关键因素。

Abstract: Large language models can generate runnable software artifacts, but their security remains difficult to evaluate end to end. This study examines that problem through a Detect--Repair--Verify (DRV) workflow, in which vulnerabilities are detected, repaired, and then rechecked with security and functional tests. It addresses four gaps in current evidence: the lack of test-grounded benchmarks for LLM-generated artifacts, limited evidence on pipeline-level effectiveness, unclear reliability of detection reports as repair guidance, and uncertain repair trustworthiness under verification.
  To support this study, EduCollab is constructed as a multi-language, multi-granularity benchmark of runnable LLM-generated web applications in PHP, JavaScript, and Python. Each artifact is paired with executable functional and exploit test suites, and the benchmark spans project-, requirement-, and file-level settings. On this benchmark, the study compares unrepaired baselines, single-pass detect--repair, and bounded iterative DRV under comparable budget constraints. Outcomes are measured by secure-and-correct yield, and intermediate artifacts and iteration traces are analyzed to assess report actionability and repair failure modes.
  The results show that bounded iterative DRV can improve secure-and-correct yield over single-pass repair, but the gains are uneven at the project level and become clearer at narrower repair scopes. Detection reports are often useful for downstream repair, but their reliability is inconsistent. Repair trustworthiness also depends strongly on repair scope. These findings highlight the need for test-grounded, end-to-end evaluation of LLM-based vulnerability management workflows.

</details>


### [4] [The Specification Gap: Coordination Failure Under Partial Knowledge in Code Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24284)
*Camilo Chacón Sartori*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 多智能体代码生成中，当多个LLM智能体独立实现同一类的不同部分时，即使规范未明确说明，它们也需要协调共享的内部表示。研究发现，随着规范细节减少，协调准确率显著下降，而更丰富的规范既是主要协调机制，也是足够的恢复工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多智能体代码生成中的协调问题：当多个基于LLM的代码智能体独立实现同一类的不同部分时，它们必须就共享的内部表示达成一致，即使规范对这些选择是隐含的。需要理解规范细节如何影响协调效果，以及协调失败的根本原因。

Method: 在51个类生成任务上进行实验，逐步剥离规范细节：从完整文档字符串(L0)到仅签名(L3)。引入对立的结构偏见（列表vs字典）来压力测试集成。使用两个Claude模型(Sonnet, Haiku)和三次独立运行。开发基于AST的冲突检测器，并进行因子恢复实验来评估不同干预措施的效果。

Result: 1. 存在持续的规范缺口：随着细节减少，双智能体集成准确率从58%降至25%，而单智能体基线从89%降至56%，留下25-39个百分点的协调缺口。
2. AST冲突检测器在最弱规范级别达到97%精度，但恢复实验显示仅恢复完整规范就能达到单智能体上限(89%)，冲突报告无额外收益。
3. 协调缺口可分解为协调成本(+16pp)和信息不对称(+11pp)，两者独立且近似可加。

Conclusion: 协调缺口不仅是隐藏信息的结果，更反映了在没有共享决策的情况下生成兼容代码的困难。结果支持多智能体代码生成的"规范优先"观点：更丰富的规范既是主要的协调机制，也是足够的恢复工具。规范细节对多智能体协调至关重要，而冲突检测虽然精确，但不如提供完整规范有效。

Abstract: When multiple LLM-based code agents independently implement parts of the same class, they must agree on shared internal representations, even when the specification leaves those choices implicit. We study this coordination problem across 51 class-generation tasks, progressively stripping specification detail from full docstrings (L0) to bare signatures (L3), and introducing opposing structural biases (lists vs. dictionaries) to stress-test integration. Three findings emerge. First, a persistent specification gap: two-agent integration accuracy drops from 58% to 25% as detail is removed, while a single-agent baseline degrades more gracefully (89% to 56%), leaving a 25--39 pp coordination gap that is consistent across two Claude models (Sonnet, Haiku) and three independent runs. Second, an AST-based conflict detector achieves 97% precision at the weakest specification level without additional LLM calls, yet a factorial recovery experiment shows that restoring the full specification alone recovers the single-agent ceiling (89%), while providing conflict reports adds no measurable benefit. Third, decomposing the gap into coordination cost (+16 pp) and information asymmetry (+11 pp) suggests that the two effects are independent and approximately additive. The gap is not merely a consequence of hidden information, but reflects the difficulty of producing compatible code without shared decisions. These results support a specification-first view of multi-agent code generation: richer specifications are both the primary coordination mechanism and the sufficient recovery instrument.

</details>


### [5] [Towards Leveraging LLMs to Generate Abstract Penetration Test Cases from Software Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23698)
*Mahdi Jafari,Rahul Sharma,Sami Naim,Christopher Gerking,Ralf Reussner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出从软件架构模型生成抽象渗透测试用例(APTCs)的方法，以支持架构级安全评估，使用大语言模型生成APTCs，评估显示高达93%有用性和86%正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件架构模型捕获影响系统质量属性（包括安全性）的早期设计决策，但实践中缺乏架构级安全评估和反馈，导致安全弱点传播到软件开发生命周期后期，最终产生易受攻击的系统。

Method: 1. 引入定义APTC概念的元模型；2. 分析相关标准和最新技术，基于两个标准设计APTC元模型：从软件架构的可推导性，以及对架构安全评估和后续渗透测试的可用性；3. 研究使用不同提示策略的大语言模型从架构模型生成有意义的APTCs。

Result: 评估显示有前景的结果，达到高达93%的有用性和86%的正确性，表明生成的APTCs能够显著支持架构师（通过突出安全关键设计决策）和渗透测试人员（通过提供可操作的测试指导）。

Conclusion: 通过从软件架构模型生成抽象渗透测试用例，成功填补了架构级安全评估的空白，为早期发现和解决安全弱点提供了有效方法，支持软件开发生命周期中的安全实践。

Abstract: Software architecture models capture early design decisions that strongly influence system quality attributes, including security. However, architecture-level security assessment and feedback are often absent in practice, allowing security weaknesses to propagate into later phases of the software development lifecycle and, in some cases, to remain undiscovered, ultimately leading to vulnerable systems. In this paper, we bridge this gap by proposing the generation of Abstract Penetration Test Cases (APTCs) from software architecture models as an input to support architecture-level security assessment. We first introduce a metamodel that defines the APTC concept, and then investigate the use of large language models with different prompting strategies to generate meaningful APTCs from architecture models. To design the APTC metamodel, we analyze relevant standards and state of the art using two criteria: (i) derivability from software architecture, and (ii) usability for both architecture security assessment and subsequent penetration testing. Building on this metamodel, we then proceed to generate APTCs from software architecture models. Our evaluation shows promising results, achieving up to 93\% usefulness and 86\% correctness, indicating that the generated APTCs can substantially support both architects (by highlighting security-critical design decisions) and penetration testers (by providing actionable testing guidance).

</details>


### [6] [Empirical Characterization of Logging Smells in Machine Learning Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23769)
*Patrick Loic Foalem,Leuson Da Silva,Foutse Khomh,Heng Li,Ettore Merlo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文对机器学习项目中日志记录异味进行了大规模实证研究，识别了12类ML特定日志异味，并通过调查验证了这些异味对可复现性、可维护性和可信度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日志记录在确保机器学习系统的可复现性、可观测性和可靠性方面起着核心作用，但设计不良的日志记录会对实验跟踪、安全性、调试和系统性能产生负面影响。目前缺乏对ML项目中日志记录质量问题的系统性研究。

Method: 1. 对444个ML仓库进行大规模分析；2. 手动标注2,448个日志异味实例；3. 基于分析识别12类ML特定日志异味；4. 对27名ML从业者进行调查，评估所识别异味的实际相关性。

Result: 1. 识别了12类日志异味，涵盖安全性、指标管理、配置、冗余度和上下文相关问题；2. 发现日志异味在ML系统中普遍存在，且在不同项目中的频率和表现形式各异；3. 调查显示大多数从业者认同所识别的异味，并报告某些类型（如记录敏感数据、指标覆盖、缺少超参数记录、无上下文记录）对可复现性、可维护性和可信度有显著影响。

Conclusion: 日志质量是ML系统工程中关键且未被充分探索的方面，研究结果为日志问题的自动检测和修复提供了机会，并公开了标注数据集以支持未来研究。

Abstract: Logging plays a central role in ensuring reproducibility, observability, and reliability in machine learning (ML) systems. While logging is generally considered a good engineering practice, poorly designed logging can negatively affect experiment tracking, security, debugging, and system performance. In this paper, we present an empirical study of logging smells in ML projects and propose a taxonomy of ML-specific logging smell types.
  We conducted a large-scale analysis of 444 ML repositories and manually labeled 2,448 instances of logging smells. Based on this analysis, we identified 12 categories of logging smells spanning security, metric management, configuration, verbosity, and context-related issues. Our results show that logging smells are widespread in ML systems and vary in frequency and manifestation across projects.
  To assess practical relevance, we conducted a survey with 27 ML practitioners. Most respondents agreed with the identified smells and reported that several types, including Logging Sensitive Data, Metric Overwrite, Missing Hyperparameter Logging, and Log Without Context, have a strong impact on reproducibility, maintainability, and trustworthiness. Other smells, such as Heavy Data Logging and Print-based Logging, were perceived as more context-dependent.
  We publicly release our labeled dataset to support future research. Our findings highlight logging quality as a critical and underexplored aspect of ML system engineering and open opportunities for automated detection and repair of logging issues.

</details>


### [7] [Bridging the Interpretation Gap in Accessibility Testing: Empathetic and Legal-Aware Bug Report Generation via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23828)
*Ryoya Koyama,Zhiyao Wang,Devi Karolita,Jialong Li,Kenji Tei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: HEAR框架将原始无障碍测试报告转化为以人为中心的叙述，通过语义切片、视觉定位、残疾角色注入和多层推理，帮助非技术利益相关者理解无障碍问题的实际影响和合规风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动应用无障碍测试工具虽然能检测界面违规，但输出多为低层次技术报告，难以让产品经理、设计师等非技术利益相关者理解实际用户伤害和合规风险，导致修复效果有限。

Method: HEAR框架首先通过语义切片和视觉定位重建UI上下文，然后为每种违规类型动态注入匹配的残疾角色，最后进行多层推理来解释物理障碍、功能阻塞以及相关法律或合规问题。

Result: 在四个流行Android应用的真实无障碍问题上评估显示，HEAR能生成事实依据充分的报告，相比原始技术日志，显著提高了感知同理心、紧迫感、说服力和法律风险意识，同时几乎不增加认知负担。

Conclusion: HEAR框架成功弥合了技术无障碍报告与非技术利益相关者之间的理解鸿沟，通过以人为中心的叙述方式有效促进了无障碍问题的修复决策和行动。

Abstract: Modern automated accessibility testing tools for mobile applications have significantly improved the detection of interface violations, yet their impact on remediation remains limited. A key reason is that existing tools typically produce low-level, technical outputs that are difficult for non-specialist stakeholders, such as product managers and designers, to interpret in terms of real user harm and compliance risk. In this paper, we present \textsc{HEAR} (\underline{H}uman-c\underline{E}ntered \underline{A}ccessibility \underline{R}eporting), a framework that bridges this interpretation gap by transforming raw accessibility bug reports into empathetic, stakeholder-oriented narratives. Given the outputs of the existing accessibility testing tool, \textsc{HEAR} first reconstructs the UI context through semantic slicing and visual grounding, then dynamically injects disability-oriented personas matched to each violation type, and finally performs multi-layer reasoning to explain the physical barrier, functional blockage, and relevant legal or compliance concerns. We evaluate the framework on real-world accessibility issues collected from four popular Android applications and conduct a user study (N=12). The results show that \textsc{HEAR} generates factually grounded reports and substantially improves perceived empathy, urgency, persuasiveness, and awareness of legal risk compared with raw technical logs, while imposing little additional cognitive burden.

</details>


### [8] [Praxium: Diagnosing Cloud Anomalies with AI-based Telemetry and Dependency Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23890)
*Rohan Kumar,Jason Li,Zongshun Zhang,Syed Mohammad Qasim,Gianluca Stringhini,Ayse Kivilcim Coskun*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Praxium是一个用于微服务异常检测和根因推断的框架，通过监控遥测数据并结合软件安装依赖信息，帮助管理员识别异常并分析其与最近软件安装的因果关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代微服务架构日益复杂，容易因配置错误和软件缺陷导致异常。传统依赖专家诊断的方法在CI/CD环境下缺乏可扩展性，且微服务部署与组件交互复杂，难以将异常行为归因于特定安装，导致解决时间延长。

Method: Praxium框架结合软件发现工具PraxiPaaS提供的依赖安装信息，持续监控遥测数据以检测异常，然后通过分析异常与最近软件安装的因果影响进行根因分析，为SRE提供相关信息。

Result: 在75次试验中使用4种合成异常，异常检测的宏F1分数始终大于0.97。因果影响分析能够可靠推断异常的正确根因，即使在软件包安装间隔越来越短的情况下也是如此。

Conclusion: Praxium能够有效进行异常检测和根因推断，论文还提供了在实际环境中有效的异常检测超参数调优分析，为微服务环境下的自动化故障诊断提供了可行方案。

Abstract: As the modern microservice architecture for cloud applications grows in popularity, cloud services are becoming increasingly complex and more vulnerable to misconfiguration and software bugs. Traditional approaches rely on expert input to diagnose and fix microservice anomalies, which lacks scalability in the face of the continuous integration and continuous deployment (CI/CD) paradigm. Microservice rollouts, containing new software installations, have complex interactions with the components of an application. Consequently, this added difficulty in attributing anomalous behavior to any specific installation or rollout results in potentially slower resolution times. To address the gaps in current diagnostic methods, this paper introduces Praxium, a framework for anomaly detection and root cause inference. Praxium aids administrators in evaluating target metric performance in the context of dependency installation information provided by a software discovery tool, PraxiPaaS. Praxium continuously monitors telemetry data to identify anomalies, then conducts root cause analysis via causal impact on recent software installations, in order to provide site reliability engineers (SRE) relevant information about an observed anomaly. In this paper, we demonstrate that Praxium is capable of effective anomaly detection and root cause inference, and we provide an analysis on effective anomaly detection hyperparameter tuning as needed in a practical setting. Across 75 total trials using four synthetic anomalies, anomaly detection consistently performs at >0.97 macro-F1. In addition, we show that causal impact analysis reliably infers the correct root cause of anomalies, even as package installations occur at increasingly shorter intervals.

</details>


### [9] [Towards Energy-aware Requirements Dependency Classification: Knowledge-Graph vs. Vector-Retrieval Augmented Inference with SLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23954)
*Shreyas Patil,Pragati Kumari,Novarun Deb,Gouri Ginde*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个能源感知框架，比较知识图谱检索（KGR）与向量语义检索（VSR）来增强7B-8B参数规模的小语言模型（SLM）在需求冲突检测中的推理能力，并系统评估了预测性能与环境影响的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统规范的持续演化需要频繁评估冲突需求，传统方法劳动密集。虽然大语言模型（LLM）在自动化检测方面有潜力，但其巨大计算需求导致能源浪费。因此需要向小语言模型（SLM）和能源感知架构转型，以实现可持续的需求工程。

Method: 提出能源感知框架，比较知识图谱检索（KGR）和向量语义检索（VSR）两种检索增强策略。通过结构化图遍历和高维语义映射提取候选需求，然后由推理引擎分类为冲突或中性。在零样本、少样本和思维链提示方法下评估这些策略。使用三支柱可持续性框架测量能耗（Wh）、延迟（s）和碳排放（gCO2eq），并结合标准准确度指标（F1分数）。

Result: 研究提供了首个系统性的经验评估和预测性能与环境影响的权衡分析。结果突出了结构化检索与语义检索在检测需求冲突方面的有效性，提供了一个可复现的、可持续性感知的架构，用于能源高效的需求工程。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了能源感知框架在小语言模型需求冲突检测中的有效性，通过比较不同检索增强策略，为可持续的需求工程提供了实证基础和架构指导，平衡了预测性能与环境影响。

Abstract: The continuous evolution of system specifications necessitates frequent evaluation of conflicting requirements, a process that is traditionally labour intensive. Although large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated significant potential for automating this detection, their massive computational requirements often result in excessive energy waste. Consequently, there is a growing need to transition toward Small Language Models (SLMs) and energy aware architectures for sustainable Requirements Engineering. This study proposes and empirically evaluates an energy aware framework that compares Knowledge Graph-based Retrieval (KGR) with Vector-based Semantic Retrieval (VSR) to enhance SLM-based inference at the 7B to 8B parameter scale. By leveraging structured graph traversal and high dimensional semantic mapping, we extract candidate requirements, which are then classified as conflicting or neutral by an inference engine. We evaluate these retrieval enhanced strategies across Zero-Shot, Few-Shot, and Chain of Thoughts prompting methods. Using a three-pillar sustainability framework measuring energy consumption (Wh), latency (s), and carbon emissions (gCO2eq) alongside standard accuracy metrics (F1 Score), this research provides a first systematic empirical evaluation and trade off analysis between predictive performance and environmental impact. Our findings highlight the effectiveness of structured versus semantic retrieval in detecting requirement conflicts, offering a reproducible, sustainability aware architecture for energy efficient requirement engineering.

</details>


### [10] [Enhancing and Reporting Robustness Boundary of Neural Code Models for Intelligent Code Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24119)
*Tingxu Han,Wei Song,Weisong Sun,Hao Wu,Chunrong Fang,Yuan Xiao,Xiaofang Zhang,Zhenyu Chen,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ENBECOME是一种针对神经代码模型的黑盒、无需训练、轻量级对抗防御方法，通过推理时引入随机语义保持扰动来平滑决策边界，同时提供经验鲁棒性增强和可证明的鲁棒性边界认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经代码模型（如CodeBERT、CodeLlama）在代码理解任务中广泛应用，但易受对抗样本攻击。现有防御方法成本高、缺乏理论鲁棒性保证，且通常需要白盒访问模型内部信息。

Method: ENBECOME是一种黑盒、无需训练、轻量级的对抗防御方法，仅在推理阶段操作。它通过向输入代码片段引入随机但语义保持的扰动来平滑神经代码模型的决策边界，从而实现经验鲁棒性增强并提供可证明的鲁棒性边界认证。

Result: 在多个神经代码模型架构和任务上的实验表明，ENBECOME显著降低了攻击成功率，同时保持了高准确率。在缺陷检测任务中，平均攻击成功率从42.43%降至9.74%，准确率仅下降0.29%。平均认证鲁棒性半径为1.63，意味着对不超过1.63个标识符的对抗性修改被证明无效。

Conclusion: ENBECOME提供了一种有效的对抗防御解决方案，既增强了神经代码模型的经验鲁棒性，又提供了可证明的鲁棒性保证，且具有黑盒、无需训练、轻量级的优势，适用于实际部署环境。

Abstract: With the development of deep learning, Neural Code Models (NCMs) such as CodeBERT and CodeLlama are widely used for code understanding tasks, including defect detection and code classification. However, recent studies have revealed that NCMs are vulnerable to adversarial examples, inputs with subtle perturbations that induce incorrect predictions while remaining difficult to detect. Existing defenses address this issue via data augmentation to empirically improve robustness, but they are costly, offer no theoretical robustness guarantees, and typically require white-box access to model internals, such as gradients. To address the above challenges, we propose ENBECOME, a novel black-box training-free and lightweight adversarial defense. ENBECOME is designed to both enhance empirical robustness and report certified robustness boundaries for NCMs. ENBECOME operates solely during inference, introducing random, semantics-preserving perturbations to input code snippets to smooth the NCM's decision boundaries. This smoothing enables ENBECOME to formally certify a robustness radius within which adversarial examples can never induce misclassification, a property known as certified robustness. We conduct comprehensive experiments across multiple NCM architectures and tasks. Results show that ENBECOME significantly reduces attack success rates while maintaining high accuracy. For example, in defect detection, it reduces the average ASR from 42.43% to 9.74% with only a 0.29% drop in accuracy. Results show that ENBECOME significantly reduces attack success rates while maintaining high accuracy. For example, in defect detection, it reduces the average ASR from 42.43% to 9.74% with only a 0.29% drop in accuracy. Furthermore, ENBECOME achieves an average certified robustness radius of 1.63, meaning that adversarial modifications to no more than 1.63 identifiers are provably ineffective.

</details>


### [11] [Towards Automated Crowdsourced Testing via Personified-LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24160)
*Shengcheng Yu,Yuchen Ling,Chunrong Fang,Zhenyu Chen,Chunyang Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PersonaTester：基于人格化LLM的自动化众包GUI测试框架，通过注入代表性人格来模拟多样化的人类测试行为


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前GUI测试面临挑战：众包测试能提供多样化的人类测试行为但缺乏可控性和可重复性，而自动化测试（特别是基于LLM的）虽具有可控性和效率优势，但缺乏人类测试者的行为多样性。需要一种能结合两者优势的方法。

Method: 提出PersonaTester框架，通过向基于LLM的智能体中注入代表性人格来模拟人类测试行为。人格定义在三个正交维度：测试心态、探索策略和交互习惯。这种方法能够在可控和可重复的方式下模拟多样化的人类测试行为。

Result: 实验结果表明：1）PersonaTester能忠实再现真实众包工作者的行为模式，表现出强的人格内一致性和清晰的人格间变异性（比基线提升117.86%-126.23%）；2）人格引导的测试智能体比无人格基线生成更有效的测试事件，触发更多崩溃（100+）和功能缺陷（11个）。

Conclusion: PersonaTester通过人格化LLM智能体成功模拟了多样化的人类测试行为，显著提升了自动化众包GUI测试的真实性和有效性，为解决自动化测试缺乏行为多样性的问题提供了创新解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation and increasing complexity of software demand robust quality assurance, with graphical user interface (GUI) testing playing a pivotal role. Crowdsourced testing has proven effective in this context by leveraging the diversity of human testers to achieve rich, scenario-based coverage across varied devices, user behaviors, and usage environments. In parallel, automated testing, particularly with the advent of large language models (LLMs), offers significant advantages in controllability, reproducibility, and efficiency, enabling scalable and systematic exploration. However, automated approaches often lack the behavioral diversity characteristic of human testers, limiting their capability to fully simulate real-world testing dynamics. To address this gap, we present PersonaTester, a novel personified-LLM-based framework designed to automate crowdsourced GUI testing. By injecting representative personas, defined along three orthogonal dimensions: testing mindset, exploration strategy, and interaction habit, into LLM-based agents, PersonaTester enables the simulation of diverse human-like testing behaviors in a controllable and repeatable manner. Experimental results demonstrate that PersonaTester faithfully reproduces the behavioral patterns of real crowdworkers, exhibiting strong intra-persona consistency and clear inter-persona variability (117.86% -- 126.23% improvement over the baseline). Moreover, persona-guided testing agents consistently generate more effective test events and trigger more crashes (100+) and functional bugs (11) than the baseline without persona, thus substantially advancing the realism and effectiveness of automated crowdsourced GUI testing.

</details>


### [12] [Detecting Underspecification in Software Requirements via k-NN Coverage Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24248)
*Wenyan Yang,Tomáš Janovec,Samantha Bavautdin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: GeoGap：一种基于几何方法检测软件需求规格中缺失需求类型的方法，通过预训练句子编码器将需求表示为单位向量，使用k近邻距离测量覆盖缺陷，在PROMISE NFR基准上达到0.935 AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件需求规格中经常存在需求类型缺失的问题，传统方法依赖人工标注或统计特征，难以有效检测完全缺失的需求类型。需要一种能够自动、准确识别规格中未包含需求类型的方法。

Method: 1. 使用预训练句子编码器将每个需求表示为单位向量；2. 通过k近邻距离测量覆盖缺陷，并进行项目级z-score归一化；3. 融合三个评分组件：逐点几何覆盖、类型限制分布覆盖、无标注总体计数；4. 通过两个超参数控制统一缺口分数。

Result: 在PROMISE NFR基准上，当项目需求数N≥50时，GeoGap检测完全缺失需求类型的AUROC达到0.935，与需要人工标注的真实计数oracle性能相当。六个基线实验证实了每个管道组件（项目级归一化、神经嵌入、几何评分）都有可测量的贡献。

Conclusion: GeoGap提供了一种有效的几何方法来自动检测软件需求规格中的缺失需求类型，无需人工标注即可达到接近真实计数的性能，为需求工程的质量保证提供了实用工具。

Abstract: We propose \geogap{}, a geometric method for detecting missing requirement types in software specifications. The method represents each requirement as a unit vector via a pretrained sentence encoder, then measures coverage deficits through $k$-nearest-neighbour distances z-scored against per-project baselines. Three complementary scoring components -- per-point geometric coverage, type-restricted distributional coverage, and annotation-free population counting -- fuse into a unified gap score controlled by two hyperparameters. On the PROMISE NFR benchmark, \geogap{} achieves 0.935 AUROC for detecting completely absent requirement types in projects with $N \geq 50$ requirements, matching a ground-truth count oracle that requires human annotation. Six baselines confirm that each pipeline component -- per-project normalisation, neural embeddings, and geometric scoring -- contributes measurable value.

</details>


### [13] [Functional Requirements for Decentralized and Self-Sovereign Identities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24250)
*Daria Schumm,Burkhard Stiller*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文针对去中心化身份（DI）和自主身份（SSI）系统缺乏可重复评估方法的问题，通过推导功能需求（FR）来建立可重复评估框架的基础步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式身份管理系统存在安全和隐私挑战，而DI/SSI系统虽具优势但采用受限，主要原因之一是缺乏可重复的方法来评估系统是否符合其承诺的质量特性。

Method: 提出操作化方法，引入形式化的功能模型，为通用的DI/SSI用例推导功能需求（FR），涵盖系统的基本操作。

Result: 建立了全面的功能需求集，可用于未来DI/SSI系统的开发和评估，为基于已建立需求工程方法的可重复评估框架奠定基础。

Conclusion: 通过系统化操作化现有非功能需求（NFR）和SSI原则，填补了DI/SSI文献中的研究空白，为可重复评估框架建立了必要的基础步骤。

Abstract: Centralized identity management systems continuously experience security and privacy challenges, motivating the exploration of Decentralized Identity (DI) and Self-Sovereign Identity (SSI) as alternatives. Despite privacy and security benefits to users, the adoption of DI/SSI systems remains limited. One contributing reason is the lack of reproducible approaches to evaluate system compliance with its promised qualities. Derivation of functional requirements (FR) is the first and necessary step to develop such an evaluation approach. Previous literature on DI/SSI significantly lacks the systematic operationalization of existing non-functional requirements (NFR) or SSI principles. This work addresses this research gap by deriving FR for a generalized DI/SSI use case, which encompasses the fundamental operations of the system. The paper details operationalization methodology, introduces a formalized functional model, and presents a comprehensive set of FR, that can be used for future development and evaluation of DI/SSI systems. As a result, establishing the fundamental step toward a reproducible evaluation framework, rooted in established requirements engineering methods.

</details>


### [14] [Software Supply Chain Smells: Lightweight Analysis for Secure Dependency Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24282)
*Larissa Schmid,Diogo Gaspar,Raphina Liu,Sofia Bobadilla,Benoit Baudry,Martin Monperrus*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出软件供应链"异味"概念作为安全风险的结构性指标，开发Dirty-Waters工具检测这些异味，通过实证研究验证其在Maven和NPM生态系统中的有效性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件系统严重依赖第三方依赖，使得软件供应链安全成为关键问题。需要结构化的指标来识别潜在安全风险，帮助开发者和组织做出明智决策

Method: 1. 提出软件供应链异味概念作为安全风险的结构性指标；2. 设计开发Dirty-Waters工具来检测这些异味；3. 通过从业者访谈验证异味与实际关注的一致性；4. 对Maven和NPM生态系统中流行软件包进行定量研究

Result: 1. 异味在Maven和NPM生态系统中普遍存在，但分布差异显著；2. Maven中可追溯性和签名问题占主导；3. NPM中大多数异味较少见，归因于注册表级别的强保证；4. 从业者访谈证实提出的异味与实际关注一致且具有价值

Conclusion: 软件供应链异味为开发者和组织提供了有价值的工具，帮助他们做出明智决策并改善软件供应链安全状况。不同生态系统需要针对性的安全策略

Abstract: Modern software systems heavily rely on third-party dependencies, making software supply chain security a critical concern. We introduce the concept of software supply chain smells as structural indicators that signal potential security risks. We design and evaluate Dirty-Waters, a novel tool for detecting such smells in the supply chains of software packages. Through interviews with practitioners, we show that our proposed smells align with real-world concerns and capture signals considered valuable. A quantitative study of popular packages in the Maven and NPM ecosystems reveals that while smells are prevalent in both, they differ significantly across ecosystems, with traceability and signing issues dominating in Maven and most smells being rare in NPM, due to strong registry-level guarantees. Software supply chain smells support developers and organizations in making informed decisions and improving their software supply chain security posture.

</details>


### [15] [Gendered Prompting and LLM Code Review: How Gender Cues in the Prompt Shape Code Quality and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24359)
*Lynn Janzen,Üveys Eroglu,Dorothea Kolossa,Pia Knöferle,Sebastian Möller,Vera Schmitt,Veronika Solopova*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究探讨性别化沟通风格如何影响LLM辅助编程和代码审查，发现性别化提示风格差异细微但可测量，但LLM代码审查存在系统性偏见，女性编写的代码更容易被批准


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地嵌入编程工作流，但性别化沟通风格如何与LLM辅助编程和代码审查相互作用尚未充分探索。研究旨在探究性别相关的语言差异是否影响代码生成结果和代码审查决策

Method: 采用混合方法试点研究：1) 分析收集的真实世界编程提示；2) 控制用户研究，开发者在LLM协助下解决相同编程任务；3) LLM模拟评估框架，系统变化性别化提示风格和审查者角色

Result: 性别相关的提示风格差异细微但可测量，女性编写的提示表现出更多间接和参与性语言，但未转化为功能正确性或静态代码质量的一致差距。LLM代码审查存在系统性偏见：平均而言，模型更倾向于批准女性编写的代码，尽管质量相当。控制实验显示性别化提示风格影响代码长度和可维护性，审查者行为在不同模型间存在差异

Conclusion: LLM辅助编程中的公平性风险主要来自LLM评估而非生成准确性，因为LLM越来越多地被部署为自动化代码审查工具。研究强调了在LLM辅助编程工作流中考虑性别偏见的重要性

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly embedded in programming workflows, from code generation to automated code review. Yet, how gendered communication styles interact with LLM-assisted programming and code review remains underexplored. We present a mixed-methods pilot study examining whether gender-related linguistic differences in prompts influence code generation outcomes and code review decisions. Across three complementary studies, we analyze (i) collected real-world coding prompts, (ii) a controlled user study, in which developers solve identical programming tasks with LLM assistance, and (iii) an LLM-based simulated evaluation framework that systematically varies gender-coded prompt styles and reviewer personas. We find that gender-related differences in prompting style are subtle but measurable, with female-authored prompts exhibiting more indirect and involved language, which does not translate into consistent gaps in functional correctness or static code quality. For LLM code review, in contrast, we observe systematic biases: on average, models approve female-authored code more, despite comparable quality. Controlled experiments show that gender-coded prompt style affect code length and maintainability, while reviewer behavior varies across models. Our findings suggest that fairness risks in LLM-assisted programming arise less from generation accuracy than from LLM evaluation, as LLMs are increasingly deployed as automated code reviewers.

</details>


### [16] [Composer 2 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24477)
*Cursor Reseach,:,Aaron Chan,Ahmed Shalaby,Alexander Wettig,Aman Sanger,Andrew Zhai,Anurag Ajay,Ashvin Nair,Charlie Snell,Chen Lu,Chen Shen,Emily Jia,Federico Cassano,Hanpeng Liu,Haoyu Chen,Henry Wildermuth,Jacob Jackson,Janet Li,Jediah Katz,Jiajun Yao,Joey Hejna,Josh Warner,Julius Vering,Kevin Frans,Lee Danilek,Less Wright,Lujing Cen,Luke Melas-Kyriazi,Michael Truell,Michiel de Jong,Naman Jain,Nate Schmidt,Nathan Wang,Niklas Muennighoff,Oleg Rybkin,Paul Loh,Phillip Kravtsov,Rishabh Yadav,Sahil Shah,Sam Kottler,Alexander M Rush,Shengtong Zhang,Shomil Jain,Sriram Sankar,Stefan Heule,Stuart H. Sul,Sualeh Asif,Victor Rong,Wanqi Zhu,William Lin,Yuchen Wu,Yuri Volkov,Yury Zemlyanskiy,Zack Holbrook,Zhiyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Composer 2是一个专门用于智能软件工程的模型，通过两阶段训练（持续预训练+大规模强化学习）提升长期规划和编码能力，在CursorBench评估中相比前代模型有显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一个专门针对智能软件工程任务的模型，能够处理长期规划和复杂编码问题，同时保持交互式使用的高效性，以解决现实世界中的软件工程挑战。

Method: 采用两阶段训练方法：1）持续预训练阶段提升模型知识和潜在编码能力；2）大规模强化学习阶段通过强化推理、准确的多步执行和长时程真实编码问题的连贯性来提升端到端编码性能。训练基础设施与部署环境保持一致，使用与真实问题匹配的环境。

Result: 在CursorBench评估中，模型相比前代Composer模型实现了重大改进（61.3）。在公开基准测试中，模型在Terminal-Bench上获得61.7分，在SWE-bench Multilingual上获得73.7分，与最先进系统相当。Composer 2展示了训练强大领域专业化模型的过程。

Conclusion: Composer 2是一个前沿级别的编码模型，通过两阶段训练方法显著提升了智能软件工程能力，特别是在长期规划和复杂编码任务方面表现出色，为训练领域专业化模型提供了有效范例。

Abstract: Composer 2 is a specialized model designed for agentic software engineering. The model demonstrates strong long-term planning and coding intelligence while maintaining the ability to efficiently solve problems for interactive use. The model is trained in two phases: first, continued pretraining to improve the model's knowledge and latent coding ability, followed by large-scale reinforcement learning to improve end-to-end coding performance through stronger reasoning, accurate multi-step execution, and coherence on long-horizon realistic coding problems. We develop infrastructure to support training in the same Cursor harness that is used by the deployed model, with equivalent tools and structure, and use environments that match real problems closely. To measure the ability of the model on increasingly difficult tasks, we introduce a benchmark derived from real software engineering problems in large codebases including our own. Composer 2 is a frontier-level coding model and demonstrates a process for training strong domain-specialized models. On our CursorBench evaluations the model achieves a major improvement in accuracy compared to previous Composer models (61.3). On public benchmarks the model scores 61.7 on Terminal-Bench and 73.7 on SWE-bench Multilingual in our harness, comparable to state-of-the-art systems.

</details>


### [17] [Efficiency for Experts, Visibility for Newcomers: A Case Study of Label-Code Alignment in Kubernetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24501)
*Matteo Vaccargiu,Sabrina Aufiero,Silvia Bartolucci,Ronnie de Souza Santos,Roberto Tonelli,Giuseppe Destefanis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究提出"标签-差异一致性"概念，分析Kubernetes项目中PR标签与修改文件的对齐程度及其对协作的影响，发现一致性普遍存在且稳定，对核心开发者可提高效率，对新手可增加参与度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GitHub等平台上的标签支持任务分类和协调，但标签与代码修改的对齐程度及其对不同经验贡献者协作的影响尚不清楚。研究旨在填补这一空白，理解标签-代码对齐如何影响开源项目的协作动态。

Method: 对Kubernetes项目进行案例研究，分析2014-2025年18,020个带有区域标签的PR及其完整文件差异。通过分析超过100万条审查评论和标签修正来验证对齐，使用分位数回归和负二项模型（按贡献者经验分层）测试与合并时间和讨论特征的关联。

Result: 标签-差异一致性普遍存在（46.6%完美对齐），多年保持稳定，审查过程中经常维护（9.2%的PR在审查中被修正）。一致性不预测合并速度但影响讨论：核心开发者（占样本81%）中，更高一致性预测更安静的审查（参与者减少18%）；一次性贡献者中，则预测更多参与（参与者增加28%）。

Conclusion: 标签-差异一致性影响审查过程中的协作展开方式，支持经验开发者的效率和新手的可见性。对于具有类似标签约定的项目，监控对齐可以帮助检测协调摩擦，并在标签和代码偏离时提供指导。

Abstract: Labels on platforms such as GitHub support triage and coordination, yet little is known about how well they align with code modifications or how such alignment affects collaboration across contributor experience levels. We present a case study of the Kubernetes project, introducing label-diff congruence - the alignment between pull request labels and modified files - and examining its prevalence, stability, behavioral validation, and relationship to collaboration outcomes across contributor tiers. We analyse 18,020 pull requests (2014--2025) with area labels and complete file diffs, validate alignment through analysis of over one million review comments and label corrections, and test associations with time-to-merge and discussion characteristics using quantile regression and negative binomial models stratified by contributor experience. Congruence is prevalent (46.6\% perfect alignment), stable over years, and routinely maintained (9.2\% of PRs corrected during review). It does not predict merge speed but shapes discussion: among core developers (81\% of the sample), higher congruence predicts quieter reviews (18\% fewer participants), whereas among one-time contributors it predicts more engagement (28\% more participants). Label-diff congruence influences how collaboration unfolds during review, supporting efficiency for experienced developers and visibility for newcomers. For projects with similar labeling conventions, monitoring alignment can help detect coordination friction and provide guidance when labels and code diverge.

</details>


### [18] [Comparing Developer and LLM Biases in Code Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24586)
*Aditya Mittal,Ryan Shar,Zichu Wu,Shyam Agarwal,Tongshuang Wu,Chris Donahue,Ameet Talwalkar,Wayne Chi,Valerie Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TRACE框架评估LLM作为代码评审法官的能力，发现其在预测人类偏好方面表现不佳，存在系统性偏见


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM越来越多地被用作代码应用中的评审法官，需要在现实的交互环境中评估其能力，这些环境应能捕捉部分上下文和模糊意图

Method: 提出TRACE框架，通过三个模态（聊天编程、IDE自动补全、指令代码编辑）评估LLM法官预测人类偏好的能力，并自动提取评分标准项以揭示系统性偏见

Result: 在13个模型中，最佳法官的表现比人类标注者差12-23%；TRACE识别出35个显著的人类与法官之间的错位来源，多数对应现有软件工程代码质量标准；在聊天编程中，法官偏向更长的代码解释而人类偏好更短的

Conclusion: LLM法官与人类偏好在现实编码应用中存在显著错位，特别是在现有代码质量维度上存在多数错配，显示LLM作为代码评审法官的局限性

Abstract: As LLMs are increasingly used as judges in code applications, they should be evaluated in realistic interactive settings that capture partial context and ambiguous intent. We present TRACE (Tool for Rubric Analysis in Code Evaluation), a framework that evaluates LLM judges' ability to predict human preferences and automatically extracts rubric items to reveal systematic biases in how humans and models weigh each item. Across three modalities -- chat-based programming, IDE autocompletion, and instructed code editing -- we use TRACE to measure how well LLM judges align with developer preferences. Among 13 different models, the best judges underperform human annotators by 12-23%. TRACE identifies 35 significant sources of misalignment between humans and judges across interaction modalities, the majority of which correspond to existing software engineering code quality criteria. For example, in chat-based coding, judges are biased towards longer code explanations while humans prefer shorter ones. We find significant misalignment on the majority of existing code quality dimensions, showing alignment gaps between LLM judges and human preference in realistic coding applications.

</details>

### [19] [PIM-CACHE: High-Efficiency Content-Aware Copy for Processing-In-Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23762)
*Peterson Yuhala,Mpoki Mwaisela,Pascal Felber,Valerio Schiavoni*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: PIM-CACHE：一种轻量级数据暂存层，通过利用工作负载相似性动态消除PIM DPU的冗余数据传输，实现内容感知复制，解决PIM架构中主机DRAM与DPU之间强制粗粒度数据传输的瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理内存（PIM）架构通过将计算靠近数据来减少传统处理器中心设计中的处理器-内存传输瓶颈。然而，这些PIM架构（如UPMEM的内存处理技术）引入了主机DRAM与DPU之间的强制粗粒度数据传输，这往往成为新的性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出PIM-CACHE，一种轻量级数据暂存层，通过利用工作负载相似性动态消除PIM DPU的冗余数据传输，实现内容感知复制（CAC）。该方法在主机DRAM和DPU之间建立缓存机制，识别和重用相似数据块。

Result: 在合成工作负载和真实基因组数据集上评估PIM-CACHE，证明其能有效减少PIM数据传输开销。实验结果显示该方法显著降低了主机DRAM与DPU之间的数据传输量。

Conclusion: PIM-CACHE通过内容感知复制机制成功解决了PIM架构中强制数据传输的瓶颈问题，为PIM系统提供了高效的数据传输优化方案，特别适用于具有工作负载相似性的应用场景。

Abstract: Processing-in-memory (PIM) architectures bring computation closer to data, reducing the processor-memory transfer bottleneck in traditional processor-centric designs. Novel hardware solutions, such as UPMEM's in-memory processing technology, achieve this by integrating low-power DRAM processing units (DPUs) into memory DIMMs, enabling massive parallelism and improved memory bandwidth. However, paradoxically, these PIM architectures introduce mandatory coarse-grained data transfers between host DRAM and DPUs, which often become the new bottleneck. We present PIM-CACHE, a lightweight data staging layer that dynamically eliminates redundant data transfers to PIM DPUs by exploiting workload similarity, achieving content-aware copy (CAC). We evaluate PIM-CACHE on both synthetic workloads and real-world genome datasets, demonstrating its effectiveness in reducing PIM data transfer overhead.

</details>


### [20] [BRIDG-Q: Barren-Plateau-Resilient Initialisation with Data-Aware LLM-Generated Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23979)
*Ngoc Nhi Nguyen,Thai T Vu,John Le,Hoa Khanh Dam,Dung Hoang Duong,Dinh Thai Hoang*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: BRIDG-Q结合LLM生成的量子电路架构与经验贝叶斯参数初始化，解决VQA中量子电路初始化的瓶颈问题，提高优化稳定性和收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电路初始化是变分量子算法中的关键瓶颈，严重影响优化稳定性和收敛性。现有方法存在局限：LLM能生成高质量电路架构但参数预测不可靠；数据驱动初始化方法（如BEINIT）假设固定ansatz模板且忽略生成电路结构。

Method: 提出BRIDG-Q神经符号管道，将LLM生成的电路架构与经验贝叶斯参数初始化相结合。使用AgentQ生成问题条件化的电路拓扑，移除生成参数，注入数据驱动的参数初始化以缓解贫瘠高原效应。

Result: 在图优化基准测试中，使用残差能量间隙和收敛指标评估，显示优化鲁棒性得到改善。数据驱动初始化对LLM生成电路仍然有效，通过oracle每实例选择实现约10%的最终残差能量减少。

Conclusion: BRIDG-Q成功弥合了LLM生成电路架构与数据驱动参数初始化之间的差距，为变分量子算法提供了更稳定和高效的初始化方法，缓解了贫瘠高原问题。

Abstract: Quantum circuit initialisation is a key bottleneck in variational quantum algorithms (VQAs), strongly impacting optimisation stability and convergence. Recent work shows that large language models (LLMs) can synthesise high-quality variational circuit architectures, but their continuous parameter predictions are unreliable. Conversely, data-driven initialisation methods such as BEINIT improve trainability via problem-adaptive priors, yet assume fixed ansatz templates and ignore generative circuit structure. We propose BRIDG-Q (Barren-Plateau-Resilient Initialisation with Data-Aware LLM-Generated Quantum Circuits), a neuro-symbolic pipeline that bridges this gap by coupling LLM-generated circuit architectures with empirical-Bayes parameter initialisation. BRIDG-Q uses AgentQ to generate problem-conditioned circuit topologies, removes generated parameters, and injects data-informed parameter initialisations to mitigate barren plateau effects. Evaluations on graph optimisation benchmarks using residual energy gap and convergence metrics show improved optimisation robustness, indicating that data-driven initialisation remains effective even for LLM-generated circuits, with oracle per-instance selection achieving approximately a 10% reduction in final residual energy.

</details>


### [21] [Novel models of computation from novel physical substrates: a bosonic example](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24531)
*Sampreet Kalita,Benjamin W. Butler,Susan Stepney,Viv Kendon*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 提出一种为特定物理设备定制计算模型和领域特定语言的方法论，并以多分量干涉仪实现的玻色子计算为例进行说明


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前非常规物理计算设备通常被用于实现传统计算模型（如布尔逻辑电路或神经形态计算），这可能导致无法充分利用设备的独特物理特性。需要开发更匹配设备物理能力的新型计算模型。

Method: 提出一种方法论，用于从物理设备的特性推导出更匹配的计算模型和领域特定语言。以多分量干涉仪实现的玻色子计算作为案例研究来展示该方法。

Result: 通过案例研究展示了如何为玻色子计算设备定制计算模型和领域特定语言，使计算模型更紧密地匹配物理设备的实际能力。

Conclusion: 为非常规物理计算设备开发定制化的计算模型和编程语言能够更好地利用设备的物理特性，避免将新型设备强行适配到传统计算范式中。

Abstract: Unconventional physical computing is producing many novel and exotic devices that can potentially be used in a computational mode. Currently, these tend to be used to implement traditional models of computation, such as boolean logic circuits, or neuromorphic approaches. This runs the risk of failing to exploit the devices to their full potential. Here we describe a methodology for deriving a model of computation and domain specific language more closely matched to a given physical device's capabilities, and illustrate it with a case study of bosonic computing as implemented by a physical multi-component interferometer.

</details>

### [22] [6D Movable Antenna for Internet of Vehicles: CSI-Free Dynamic Antenna Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23991)
*Maoxin Ji,Qiong Wu,Pingyi Fan,Kezhi Wang,Wen Chen,Khaled B. Letaief*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出一种用于车联网的6维可移动天线动态配置方法，通过车辆运动预测和离线方向响应先验，无需实时信道状态信息即可优化天线位置和方向


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车联网场景中车辆高速移动导致信道快速时空变化，给6维可移动天线的实时优化带来巨大挑战，需要低复杂度、无需瞬时CSI的动态配置方法

Method: 结合车辆运动预测和离线方向响应先验，在每个重配置时刻优化天线位置和方向，最大化未来时间窗口内的平均和速率

Result: 在城市交叉口场景的仿真中，所提6DMA方案在总速率方面显著优于传统固定天线阵列和简化的6DMA基线方案

Conclusion: 该工作首次将6DMA应用于车联网，提出的低复杂度、无需瞬时CSI的动态配置方法能有效应对车辆高速移动带来的信道快速变化挑战

Abstract: Deploying six-dimensional movable antenna (6DMA) systems in Internet-of-Vehicles (IoV) scenarios can greatly enhance spectral efficiency. However, the high mobility of vehicles causes rapid spatio-temporal channel variations, posing a significant challenge to real-time 6DMA optimization. In this work, we pioneer the application of 6DMA in IoV and propose a low-complexity, instantaneous channel state information (CSI)-free dynamic configuration method. By integrating vehicle motion prediction with offline directional response priors, the proposed approach optimizes antenna positions and orientations at each reconfiguration epoch to maximize the average sum rate over a future time window. Simulation results in a typical urban intersection scenario demonstrate that the proposed 6DMA scheme significantly outperforms conventional fixed antenna arrays and simplified 6DMA baseline schemes in terms of total sum rate.

</details>

### [23] [Semantics for 2D Rasterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23696)
*Bhargav Kulkarni,Henry Whiting,Pavel Panchekha*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: μSkia：Skia 2D图形库的形式化语义，用于验证和优化Chrome等应用中的低效光栅化代码


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Skia等光栅化库本身高度优化，但应用程序（如Chrome）仍会产生低效的操作序列，原因是这些库具有复杂语义和不透明的执行模型

Method: 1. 为Skia库开发形式化语义μSkia，并在Lean中机械化实现；2. 识别Chrome产生的四种低效Skia代码模式并编写优化版本；3. 开发高性能Skia优化器自动应用这些模式；4. 通过μSkia验证优化正确性，包括识别复杂边界条件

Result: 在从Top 100网站收集的99个Skia程序中，优化器相比Skia最新GPU后端实现了18.7%的加速，优化时间最多仅32μs。加速效果在不同网站、Skia后端和GPU上均保持稳定

Conclusion: μSkia为Skia提供了形式化语义基础，能够验证优化正确性并显著提升实际应用性能，同时通过翻译验证确保端到端正确性

Abstract: Rasterization is the process of determining the color of every pixel drawn by an application. Powerful rasterization libraries like Skia, CoreGraphics, and Direct2D put exceptional effort into drawing, blending, and rendering efficiently. Yet applications are still hindered by the inefficient sequences of operations that they ask these libraries to perform. Even Google Chrome, a highly optimized program co-developed with the Skia rasterization library, still produces inefficient instruction sequences even on the top 100 most visited websites. The underlying reason for this inefficiency is that rasterization libraries have complex semantics and opaque and non-obvious execution models.
  To address this issue, we introduce $μ$Skia, a formal semantics for the Skia 2D graphics library, and mechanize this semantics in Lean. $μ$Skia covers language and graphics features like canvas state, the layer stack, blending, and color filters, and the semantics itself is split into three strata to separate concerns and enable extensibility. We then identify four patterns of sub-optimal Skia code produced by Google Chrome, and then write replacements for each pattern. $μ$Skia allows us to verify the replacements are correct, including identifying numerous tricky side conditions. We then develop a high-performance Skia optimizer that applies these patterns to speed up rasterization. On 99 Skia programs gathered from the top 100 websites, this optimizer yields a speedup of 18.7% over Skia's most modern GPU backend, while taking at most 32 $μ$s for optimization. The speedups persist across a variety of websites, Skia backends, and GPUs. To provide true, end-to-end verification, optimization traces produced by the optimizer are loaded back into the $μ$Skia semantics and translation validated in Lean.

</details>


### [24] [Adapting the MVVM pattern to C++ frontends and Agda-based backends](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24199)
*Viktor Csimma*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种使用Agda和Haskell开发Qt应用程序的通用方法，解决了在Agda代码中使用任意Haskell内置库的问题，并提供了用户中断处理的新方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然已有使用agda2hs和Haskell FFI绑定在C++中编写Qt应用程序的方法，但缺乏可重复的通用方法论，且无法在Agda代码中使用任意的Haskell内置库。

Method: 采用Model-View-ViewModel架构思想，将模型部分用函数式语言实现，提供软件开发工具包实现简易安装和自动化编译。针对使用任意Haskell内置库的问题，提出并比较了多种解决方案，并设计了支持任意中断和通过C/C++直接FFI调用中断的新型Haskell future机制。

Result: 通过基准测试证明，作为该方法论基础的agda2hs编译器与Rocq的OCaml提取功能和Agda默认的MAlonzo后端相比具有可行性。提供了使用任意Haskell内置库的解决方案比较，以及支持任意中断和C/C++直接FFI调用中断的新型Haskell future设计。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一套完整的、文档齐全的通用方法论，用于在Qt应用程序开发中集成Agda和Haskell，解决了关键技术障碍，并通过基准测试验证了agda2hs编译器的实用性，为函数式语言在GUI开发中的应用提供了新的途径。

Abstract: Using agda2hs and ad-hoc Haskell FFI bindings, writing Qt applications in C++ with Agda- or Haskell-based backends (possibly including correctness proofs) is already possible. However, there was no repeatable methodology to do so, nor to use \emph{arbitrary} Haskell built-in libraries in Agda code.
  We present a well-documented, general methodology to address this, applying the ideas of the Model-View-ViewModel architecture to models implemented in functional languages. This is augmented by a software development kit providing easy installation and automated compilation.
  For obstacles arising, we provide solutions and ideas that are novel contributions by themselves. We describe and compare solutions for using arbitrary Haskell built-ins in Agda code, highlighting their advantages and disadvantages. Also, for user interruption, we present a new Haskell future design that, to the best of our knowledge, is the first to provide for arbitrary interruption and the first to provide for interruption via direct FFI calls from C and C++.
  Finally, we prove with benchmarks that the agda2hs compiler at the base of our methodology is viable when compared to other solutions, specifically to the OCaml extraction feature of Rocq and the default MAlonzo backend of Agda.

</details>


### [25] [DVM: Real-Time Kernel Generation for Dynamic AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24239)
*Jingzhi Fang,Xiong Gao,Renwei Zhang,Zichun Ye,Lei Chen,Jie Zhao,Chengnuo Huang,Hui Xu,Xuefeng Jin*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: DVM是一个实时编译器，通过字节码虚拟机和运行时操作符编译技术，解决了动态AI模型（如动态张量形状和控制流）的编译效率问题，相比现有方案在模型效率和编译时间上均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI计算中普遍存在动态性（如动态张量形状、动态控制流），现有运行时编译方案编译时间长损害模型效率，而离线编译器要么需要覆盖所有可能执行实例导致编译时间和设备内存开销大，要么为了可用性牺牲优化机会。

Method: 提出实时编译器DVM：1）基于字节码虚拟机的运行时操作符编译器，将操作符程序编码为CPU上的字节码，解码为NPU上的虚拟指令直接执行；2）操作符融合器，在静态图上执行基于符号推导的融合，在动态图上执行运行时融合，支持模式和堆叠式融合以增加融合机会。

Result: 在操作符、子图和模型上的评估显示，相比TorchInductor、PyTorch-eager和MindSpore-graph-O0，DVM在操作符/模型效率上最高提升11.77倍，最大编译时间快5个数量级。

Conclusion: DVM通过运行时编译和字节码虚拟机技术，有效解决了动态AI模型的编译效率问题，实现了实时编译和高性能执行，为动态模型的高效部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Dynamism is common in AI computation, e.g., the dynamic tensor shapes and the dynamic control flows in models. Due to the long compilation time, existing runtime compilation damages the model efficiency, while the offline compilers either suffer from the long compilation time and device memory footprint to cover all the possible execution instances of a dynamic model, or sacrifice optimization opportunities for usability. In this paper, we rethink the feasibility of runtime compilation for dynamic models and identify that the key for it to work is to speed up the compilation or hide the compilation overhead. To do this, we propose a real-time compiler, DVM. In DVM, we design a runtime operator compiler based on a bytecode virtual machine to perform effective and efficient compilation for each dynamic operator instance given its input. Specifically, instead of compiling programs into machine code, we encode the operator program into bytecode on the CPU and decode the bytecode into virtual instructions for direct execution on the NPU. Based on the runtime operator compiler, we further propose an operator fuser, which performs symbol-deduction-based fusion on static graphs and runtime fusion on dynamic graphs. Both pattern- and stacking-based fusion are supported to increase fusion opportunities. Evaluation on operators, subgraphs, and models shows that, compared with TorchInductor, PyTorch-eager and MindSpore-graph-O0, we are up to 11.77$\times$ better in terms of the operator/model efficiency and up to 5 orders of magnitude faster in terms of the maximum compilation time.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

### [26] [Beyond Accuracy: Introducing a Symbolic-Mechanistic Approach to Interpretable Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23517)
*Reza Habibi,Darian Lee,Magy Seif El-Nasr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出机制感知评估方法，结合符号规则与机制可解释性来区分真实泛化与记忆化等捷径，并在NL-to-SQL任务中验证了传统准确率评估的局限性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于准确率的评估方法无法可靠区分真实泛化与记忆化、数据泄漏或脆弱启发式等捷径，特别是在小数据场景下。现有评估指标可能错误地暗示模型能力，掩盖其实际缺陷。

Method: 提出机制感知评估框架，结合任务相关的符号规则与机制可解释性技术，生成算法化的通过/失败分数。通过NL-to-SQL任务进行验证：训练两个相同架构的模型，一个无模式信息（强制记忆化），一个有模式信息（支持基础理解），然后对比分析。

Result: 传统评估显示记忆化模型在未见数据上达到94%字段名准确率，错误暗示其能力。而符号-机制评估揭示该模型违反了核心模式泛化规则，这种失败在准确率指标中完全不可见。

Conclusion: 机制感知评估能够可靠揭示模型是否真正泛化而非利用捷径，为模型评估提供了更严谨的方法论，特别是在区分真实理解与表面模式匹配方面具有重要价值。

Abstract: Accuracy-based evaluation cannot reliably distinguish genuine generalization from shortcuts like memorization, leakage, or brittle heuristics, especially in small-data regimes. In this position paper, we argue for mechanism-aware evaluation that combines task-relevant symbolic rules with mechanistic interpretability, yielding algorithmic pass/fail scores that show exactly where models generalize versus exploit patterns. We demonstrate this on NL-to-SQL by training two identical architectures under different conditions: one without schema information (forcing memorization), one with schema (enabling grounding). Standard evaluation shows the memorization model achieves 94% field-name accuracy on unseen data, falsely suggesting competence. Our symbolic-mechanistic evaluation reveals this model violates core schema generalization rules, a failure invisible to accuracy metrics.

</details>


### [27] [Implicit Turn-Wise Policy Optimization for Proactive User-LLM Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23550)
*Haoyu Wang,Yuxin Chen,Liang Luo,Buyun Zhang,Ellie Dingqiao Wen,Pan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ITPO通过隐式过程奖励模型从稀疏结果信号中推导细粒度轮次奖励，优化多轮人机协作任务，相比现有基线实现更好收敛和语义对齐


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮人机协作在自适应教学、对话推荐和专业咨询等交互服务中至关重要，但强化学习优化面临中间奖励稀疏和用户响应随机性高的挑战

Method: 提出隐式轮次策略优化（ITPO），利用隐式过程奖励模型从稀疏结果信号中推导细粒度的轮次过程奖励，相比波动性大的token级奖励，轮次级信号更鲁棒，可通过归一化机制增强训练稳定性

Result: 在数学辅导、文档撰写和医疗推荐三个代表性多轮协作任务上评估ITPO，与PPO、GRPO或RLOO结合时，相比现有基线实现更好收敛；轨迹分析确认ITPO推断的轮次偏好与人类判断语义对齐

Conclusion: ITPO通过隐式过程奖励模型有效解决多轮人机协作中的奖励稀疏问题，提供鲁棒的轮次级奖励信号，改善强化学习训练效果，代码已开源

Abstract: Multi-turn human-AI collaboration is fundamental to deploying interactive services such as adaptive tutoring, conversational recommendation, and professional consultation. However, optimizing these interactions via reinforcement learning is hindered by the sparsity of verifiable intermediate rewards and the high stochasticity of user responses. To address these challenges, we introduce Implicit Turn-wise Policy Optimization (ITPO). ITPO leverages an implicit process reward model to derive fine-grained, turn-wise process rewards from sparse outcome signals. Unlike volatile token-level rewards, these turn-level signals exhibit superior robustness and may utilize a normalization mechanism to further enhance training stability. We evaluate ITPO across three representative multi-turn collaborative tasks: math tutoring, document writing, and medical recommendation. Empirical results demonstrate that ITPO, when combined with PPO, GRPO, or RLOO, consistently achieves improved convergence than existing baselines. Elaborate trajectory analysis confirms that ITPO infers turn-wise preferences that are semantically aligned with human judgment. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/Graph-COM/ITPO.

</details>


### [28] [Upper Entropy for 2-Monotone Lower Probabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23558)
*Tuan-Anh Vu,Sébastien Destercke,Frédéric Pichon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文对概率集不确定性建模中的上熵计算问题进行了全面的算法和复杂性分析，证明了该问题存在强多项式解，并针对2-单调下概率及其特例提出了比以往算法显著的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不确定性量化在模型选择/正则化、量化预测不确定性以进行主动学习或OOD检测等任务中至关重要。在将不确定性建模为概率集的信度方法中，上熵作为不确定性度量起着核心作用。然而，上熵的计算问题需要更深入的分析和改进。

Method: 本文专注于上熵的计算方面，提供了该问题的详尽算法和复杂性分析。特别地，证明了该问题具有强多项式解，并针对2-单调下概率及其特例提出了多项显著改进的算法。

Result: 研究结果表明上熵计算问题存在强多项式时间算法解，并且提出的新算法在2-单调下概率及其特例的计算效率上相比以往算法有显著提升。

Conclusion: 本文为上熵计算提供了坚实的理论基础和高效算法，解决了概率集不确定性建模中的关键计算问题，为实际应用中的不确定性量化提供了更可靠的工具。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification is a key aspect in many tasks such as model selection/regularization, or quantifying prediction uncertainties to perform active learning or OOD detection. Within credal approaches that consider modeling uncertainty as probability sets, upper entropy plays a central role as an uncertainty measure. This paper is devoted to the computational aspect of upper entropies, providing an exhaustive algorithmic and complexity analysis of the problem. In particular, we show that the problem has a strongly polynomial solution, and propose many significant improvements over past algorithms proposed for 2-monotone lower probabilities and their specific cases.

</details>


### [29] [Synthetic Mixed Training: Scaling Parametric Knowledge Acquisition Beyond RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23562)
*Seungju Han,Konwoo Kim,Chanwoo Park,Benjamin Newman,Suhas Kotha,Jaehun Jung,James Zou,Yejin Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Synthetic Mixed Training方法，结合合成QA和合成文档，突破RAG性能天花板，在多个长文档理解基准上超越RAG性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成数据增强方法在数据受限领域存在局限性，单纯增加合成数据量或使用更强生成器只能获得递减收益，无法超越检索增强生成(RAG)的性能天花板

Method: 提出Synthetic Mixed Training：1) 结合合成QA和合成文档的互补训练信号；2) 引入Focal Rewriting技术，显式基于特定问题生成文档，提高合成文档多样性；3) 实现对数线性改进，随合成数据量和生成器强度增加而提升

Result: 在QuaLITY长文档阅读理解基准上，Llama 8B模型相对RAG提升2.6%；使用Focal Rewriting后提升至4.4%；在QuaLITY、LongHealth、FinanceBench三个基准的六个设置中，五个设置超越RAG，平均提升2.6%；与RAG结合时获得9.1%增益

Conclusion: Synthetic Mixed Training通过结合合成QA和合成文档的互补训练信号，有效突破RAG性能限制，在数据受限领域实现显著性能提升，为语言模型知识学习提供了有效方法

Abstract: Synthetic data augmentation helps language models learn new knowledge in data-constrained domains. However, naively scaling existing synthetic data methods by training on more synthetic tokens or using stronger generators yields diminishing returns below the performance of RAG. To break the RAG ceiling, we introduce Synthetic Mixed Training, which combines synthetic QAs and synthetic documents. This leverages their complementary training signals, and enables log-linear improvements as both synthetic data volume and generator strength increase. This allows the model to outperform RAG by a 2.6\% relative gain on QuaLITY, a long-document reading comprehension benchmark. In addition, we introduce Focal Rewriting, a simple technique for synthetic document generation that explicitly conditions document generation on specific questions, improving the diversity of synthetic documents and yielding a steeper log-linear scaling curve. On QuaLITY, our final recipe trains a Llama 8B model that outperforms RAG by 4.4\% relatively. Across models and benchmarks (QuaLITY, LongHealth, FinanceBench), our training enables models to beat RAG in five of six settings, outperforms by 2.6\%, and achieves a 9.1\% gain when combined with RAG.

</details>


### [30] [Likelihood hacking in probabilistic program synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24126)
*Jacek Karwowski,Younesse Kaddar,Zihuiwen Ye,Nikolay Malkin,Sam Staton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了"似然黑客攻击"概念，即语言模型通过生成未归一化的概率程序来人为提高边际似然奖励，而非真正改善数据拟合。作者形式化了这一问题，提出了防止LH的安全语言片段，并开发了SafeStan来实证验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当语言模型通过强化学习训练生成概率程序时，它们可能通过产生数据分布未归一化的程序来人为提高边际似然奖励，而不是真正改善数据拟合。这种"似然黑客攻击"行为破坏了自动贝叶斯模型发现的可靠性，需要从语言层面加以防范。

Method: 1. 在核心概率编程语言中形式化似然黑客攻击问题；2. 提出防止LH的充分语法条件，证明满足这些条件的安全语言片段$\mathcal{L}_{\text{safe}}$不会产生LH程序；3. 实证展示GRPO训练模型生成PyMC代码时快速发现LH漏洞；4. 实现$\mathcal{L}_{\text{safe}}$条件为SafeStan，验证其在优化压力下防止LH的有效性。

Result: 1. GRPO训练模型在最初几步训练中就发现了LH漏洞，违规率显著高于未训练模型基线；2. SafeStan成功防止了优化压力下的LH行为；3. 语言级安全约束在理论和实践上都被证明对自动贝叶斯模型发现是有效且必要的。

Conclusion: 语言级安全约束对于防止概率编程中的似然黑客攻击既是理论上有依据的，也是实践中有效的。SafeStan作为Stan的LH抵抗修改版本，为自动贝叶斯模型发现提供了可靠的编程语言基础，确保了模型评估的完整性。

Abstract: When language models are trained by reinforcement learning (RL) to write probabilistic programs, they can artificially inflate their marginal-likelihood reward by producing programs whose data distribution fails to normalise instead of fitting the data better. We call this failure likelihood hacking (LH). We formalise LH in a core probabilistic programming language (PPL) and give sufficient syntactic conditions for its prevention, proving that a safe language fragment $\mathcal{L}_{\text{safe}}$ satisfying these conditions cannot produce likelihood-hacking programs. Empirically, we show that GRPO-trained models generating PyMC code discover LH exploits within the first few training steps, driving violation rates well above the untrained-model baseline. We implement $\mathcal{L}_{\text{safe}}$'s conditions as $\texttt{SafeStan}$, a LH-resistant modification of Stan, and show empirically that it prevents LH under optimisation pressure. These results show that language-level safety constraints are both theoretically grounded and effective in practice for automated Bayesian model discovery.

</details>


### [31] [Large Language Model Guided Incentive Aware Reward Design for Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24324)
*Dogan Urgun,Gokhan Gungor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 基于LLM的自动化奖励设计框架，通过环境信息合成可执行的奖励程序，在有限计算预算下提升多智能体协作性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手动设计多智能体协作系统的辅助奖励存在困难，不当的奖励可能导致次优协调，特别是在稀疏任务反馈无法提供足够指导的情况下

Method: 提出自动化奖励设计框架：1) 利用大语言模型从环境信息中合成可执行的奖励程序；2) 将候选程序约束在形式化有效性范围内；3) 在固定计算预算下从头训练策略评估程序效果；4) 仅依赖稀疏任务回报进行选择

Result: 在四个不同Overcooked-AI布局中评估：1) 迭代搜索代际持续产生更优的任务回报和交付数量；2) 在交互瓶颈主导的环境中增益最为显著；3) 合成的塑造组件分析显示动作选择相互依赖性增加，协调密集型任务中信号对齐改善

Conclusion: 基于目标的奖励程序搜索可以减轻手动工程负担，同时产生与有限预算下协作学习兼容的塑造信号，为多智能体系统自动化奖励设计提供了有效途径

Abstract: Designing effective auxiliary rewards for cooperative multi-agent systems remains a precarious task; misaligned incentives risk inducing suboptimal coordination, especially where sparse task feedback fails to provide sufficient grounding. This study introduces an automated reward design framework that leverages large language models to synthesize executable reward programs from environment instrumentation. The procedure constrains candidate programs within a formal validity envelope and evaluates their efficacy by training policies from scratch under a fixed computational budget; selection depends exclusively on the sparse task return. The framework is evaluated across four distinct Overcooked-AI layouts characterized by varied corridor congestion, handoff dependencies, and structural asymmetries. Iterative search generations consistently yield superior task returns and delivery counts, with the most pronounced gains occurring in environments dominated by interaction bottlenecks. Diagnostic analysis of the synthesized shaping components indicates increased interdependence in action selection and improved signal alignment in coordination-intensive tasks. These results demonstrate that the search for objectivegrounded reward programs can mitigate the burden of manual engineering while producing shaping signals compatible with cooperative learning under finite budgets.

</details>


### [32] [Conformalized Transfer Learning for Li-ion Battery State of Health Forecasting under Manufacturing and Usage Variability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24475)
*Samuel Filgueira da Silva,Mehmet Fatih Ozkan,Faissal El Idrissi,Marcello Canova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种结合LSTM、MMD域适应和CP不确定性量化的迁移学习框架，用于提高锂离子电池健康状态预测的泛化能力和可信度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于实验室特定条件校准的SOH预测模型难以泛化到存在制造差异或不同运行条件的新电池，需要解决领域偏移问题并提高预测可信度

Method: 结合LSTM模型、MMD域适应和CP不确定性量化：1）在虚拟电池数据集上训练LSTM；2）使用MMD对齐模拟域与目标域的潜在特征分布；3）通过CP提供校准的、分布无关的预测区间

Result: 该框架提高了SOH预测在异构电池间的泛化能力和可信度，能够处理制造差异和运行条件变化带来的领域偏移问题

Conclusion: 提出的不确定性感知迁移学习框架通过域适应和不确定性量化，有效解决了锂离子电池SOH预测的泛化挑战，为电池安全可靠运行提供了更可信的预测工具

Abstract: Accurate forecasting of state-of-health (SOH) is essential for ensuring safe and reliable operation of lithium-ion cells. However, existing models calibrated on laboratory tests at specific conditions often fail to generalize to new cells that differ due to small manufacturing variations or operate under different conditions. To address this challenge, an uncertainty-aware transfer learning framework is proposed, combining a Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) model with domain adaptation via Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD) and uncertainty quantification through Conformal Prediction (CP). The LSTM model is trained on a virtual battery dataset designed to capture real-world variability in electrode manufacturing and operating conditions. MMD aligns latent feature distributions between simulated and target domains to mitigate domain shift, while CP provides calibrated, distribution-free prediction intervals. This framework improves both the generalization and trustworthiness of SOH forecasts across heterogeneous cells.

</details>


### [33] [Towards Safe Learning-Based Non-Linear Model Predictive Control through Recurrent Neural Network Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24503)
*Mihaela-Larisa Clement,Mónika Farsang,Agnes Poks,Johannes Edelmann,Manfred Plöchl,Radu Grosu,Ezio Bartocci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Sequential-AMPC方法，通过跨预测时域共享参数的序列神经策略生成MPC候选控制序列，结合安全增强在线评估和回退机制，减少专家数据需求和训练成本，提高闭环安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）在实际部署中受限于在线计算复杂度，特别是在嵌入式硬件上求解非线性规划问题代价高昂。基于学习的NMPC近似方法虽然将计算转移到离线，但通常需要大量专家数据集和昂贵的训练。

Method: 提出Sequential-AMPC序列神经策略，通过跨预测时域共享参数生成MPC候选控制序列。部署时，将策略包装在安全增强的在线评估和回退机制中，形成Safe Sequential-AMPC。

Result: 相比前馈策略基线，Sequential-AMPC需要显著更少的专家MPC轨迹，生成的候选序列具有更高的可行性率和改进的闭环安全性。在高维系统上，表现出更好的学习动态和更少的训练轮次，同时保持稳定的验证改进，而前馈基线可能停滞。

Conclusion: Sequential-AMPC提供了一种高效的学习型NMPC近似方法，通过序列神经策略和在线安全机制，在减少专家数据需求和训练成本的同时，提高了控制性能和安全性，特别适用于复杂模型和长时域的高维系统。

Abstract: The practical deployment of nonlinear model predictive control (NMPC) is often limited by online computation: solving a nonlinear program at high control rates can be expensive on embedded hardware, especially when models are complex or horizons are long. Learning-based NMPC approximations shift this computation offline but typically demand large expert datasets and costly training. We propose Sequential-AMPC, a sequential neural policy that generates MPC candidate control sequences by sharing parameters across the prediction horizon. For deployment, we wrap the policy in a safety-augmented online evaluation and fallback mechanism, yielding Safe Sequential-AMPC. Compared to a naive feedforward policy baseline across several benchmarks, Sequential-AMPC requires substantially fewer expert MPC rollouts and yields candidate sequences with higher feasibility rates and improved closed-loop safety. On high-dimensional systems, it also exhibits better learning dynamics and performance in fewer epochs while maintaining stable validation improvement where the feedforward baseline can stagnate.

</details>


### [34] [StateLinFormer: Stateful Training Enhancing Long-term Memory in Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23571)
*Zhiyuan Chen,Yuxuan Zhong,Fan Wang,Bo Yu,Pengtao Shao,Shaoshan Liu,Ning Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StateLinFormer：一种具有状态记忆机制的线性注意力导航模型，通过跨训练段保持循环记忆状态实现长时记忆保留，显著优于无状态线性注意力模型和固定上下文窗口的Transformer基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航系统面临两难：模块化系统依赖显式映射但缺乏灵活性，而基于Transformer的端到端模型受限于固定上下文窗口，无法在扩展交互中保持持久记忆。需要一种既能实现即时泛化又能支持持续适应的长时记忆机制。

Method: 提出StateLinFormer，一种线性注意力导航模型，采用状态记忆训练机制。该机制在连续训练段之间保持循环记忆状态，而不是在每个批次边界重新初始化。这种训练范式有效近似于在无限长序列上的学习，使模型能够实现长时程记忆保留。

Result: 在MAZE和ProcTHOR环境中的实验表明，StateLinFormer显著优于其无状态线性注意力对应模型和具有固定上下文窗口的标准Transformer基线。随着交互长度增加，持久状态训练大幅改善了上下文依赖的适应能力，表明模型在导航任务中的上下文学习能力得到增强。

Conclusion: StateLinFormer通过状态记忆训练机制解决了导航系统中长时记忆保留的挑战，实现了对无限长序列的有效近似学习。该方法不仅提升了导航性能，还增强了模型的上下文学习能力，为智能导航系统的发展提供了新方向。

Abstract: Effective navigation intelligence relies on long-term memory to support both immediate generalization and sustained adaptation. However, existing approaches face a dilemma: modular systems rely on explicit mapping but lack flexibility, while Transformer-based end-to-end models are constrained by fixed context windows, limiting persistent memory across extended interactions. We introduce StateLinFormer, a linear-attention navigation model trained with a stateful memory mechanism that preserves recurrent memory states across consecutive training segments instead of reinitializing them at each batch boundary. This training paradigm effectively approximates learning on infinitely long sequences, enabling the model to achieve long-horizon memory retention. Experiments across both MAZE and ProcTHOR environments demonstrate that StateLinFormer significantly outperforms its stateless linear-attention counterpart and standard Transformer baselines with fixed context windows. Notably, as interaction length increases, persistent stateful training substantially improves context-dependent adaptation, suggesting an enhancement in the model's In-Context Learning (ICL) capabilities for navigation tasks.

</details>


### [35] [Dual-Criterion Curriculum Learning: Application to Temporal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23573)
*Gaspard Abel,Eloi Campagne,Mohamed Benloughmari,Argyris Kalogeratos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DCCL框架结合损失和密度双重标准评估样本难度，在时间序列预测任务中优于仅使用损失或非课程学习的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 课程学习(CL)中定义有意义的难度评估标准是关键瓶颈，现有启发式方法通常仅适用于特定应用。需要更通用的难度评估框架。

Method: 提出双标准课程学习(DCCL)框架，结合损失标准（基于训练证据）和密度标准（在数据表示空间中学习）。DCCL校准训练证据时考虑数据稀疏性会放大学习难度。

Result: 在多变量时间序列基准测试中，基于密度的和混合双标准课程在One-Pass和Baby-Steps训练计划下优于仅使用损失的基线和标准非课程学习。

Conclusion: DCCL框架通过结合损失和密度双重标准有效评估样本难度，在时间序列预测任务中展示了优于传统方法的性能。

Abstract: Curriculum Learning (CL) is a meta-learning paradigm that trains a model by feeding the data instances incrementally according to a schedule, which is based on difficulty progression. Defining meaningful difficulty assessment measures is crucial and most usually the main bottleneck for effective learning, while also in many cases the employed heuristics are only application-specific. In this work, we propose the Dual-Criterion Curriculum Learning (DCCL) framework that combines two views of assessing instance-wise difficulty: a loss-based criterion is complemented by a density-based criterion learned in the data representation space. Essentially, DCCL calibrates training-based evidence (loss) under the consideration that data sparseness amplifies the learning difficulty. As a testbed, we choose the time-series forecasting task. We evaluate our framework on multivariate time-series benchmarks under standard One-Pass and Baby-Steps training schedules. Empirical results show the interest of density-based and hybrid dual-criterion curricula over loss-only baselines and standard non-CL training in this setting.

</details>


### [36] [APreQEL: Adaptive Mixed Precision Quantization For Edge LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23575)
*Meriem Bouzouad,Yuan-Hao Chang,Jalil Boukhobza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应混合精度量化机制，针对边缘设备部署LLM时平衡内存、延迟和精度，通过分析层间贡献和硬件行为为每层分配最合适的量化类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在推理、代码生成等任务上表现出色，但计算成本和内存需求高，难以在边缘设备上部署以实现实时响应和数据隐私。现有量化方法通常对所有层采用统一精度，未考虑不同层对精度降低的敏感度差异，且内存消耗与计算吞吐量不一定对齐。

Method: 提出自适应混合精度量化机制：1) 分析层间贡献；2) 推断不同量化类型在目标硬件平台上的行为；3) 根据用户定义的优先级平衡内存、延迟和精度；4) 为每层分配最合适的量化类型，确保层重要性和整体性能权衡得到联合考虑。

Result: 该方法实现了统一量化无法达到的新配置设计，扩展了在资源受限设备上高效部署LLM的解决方案空间。

Conclusion: 自适应混合精度量化机制能够更好地平衡边缘设备部署中的内存、延迟和精度需求，通过考虑层间差异和硬件特性，为每层选择最优量化策略，从而在资源受限环境下实现高效LLM部署。

Abstract: Today, large language models have demonstrated their strengths in various tasks ranging from reasoning, code generation, and complex problem solving. However, this advancement comes with a high computational cost and memory requirements, making it challenging to deploy these models on edge devices to ensure real-time responses and data privacy. Quantization is one common approach to reducing memory use, but most methods apply it uniformly across all layers. This does not account for the fact that different layers may respond differently to reduced precision. Importantly, memory consumption and computational throughput are not necessarily aligned, further complicating deployment decisions. This paper proposes an adaptive mixed precision quantization mechanism that balances memory, latency, and accuracy in edge deployment under user-defined priorities. This is achieved by analyzing the layer-wise contribution and by inferring how different quantization types behave across the target hardware platform in order to assign the most suitable quantization type to each layer. This integration ensures that layer importance and the overall performance trade-offs are jointly respected in this design. Our work unlocks new configuration designs that uniform quantization cannot achieve, expanding the solution space to efficiently deploy the LLMs on resource-constrained devices.

</details>


### [37] [The Geometric Price of Discrete Logic: Context-driven Manifold Dynamics of Number Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23577)
*Long Zhang,Dai-jun Lin,Wei-neng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLMs通过非等距拓扑变形实现逻辑推理，而非线性等距投影。任务上下文作为动态算子驱动双重调制机制：类无关拓扑保持和特定代数发散，形成离散决策边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在连续语义空间上平滑泛化，但严格逻辑推理需要形成离散决策边界。现有基于线性等距投影的理论无法解决这一根本矛盾，需要新的几何解释框架。

Method: 采用Gram-Schmidt分解分析残差流激活，揭示双重调制机制：类无关拓扑保持和特定代数发散。通过向量消融实验建立因果关系，验证几何演化过程，并分析层间动态和社会压力下的表现。

Result: 1. 代数发散成分的消融使奇偶性分类准确率从100%降至机会水平(38.57%)；2. 发现三层几何动态模式；3. 社会压力下模型无法产生足够发散，导致"流形纠缠"，几何解释奉承和幻觉现象。

Conclusion: LLMs中离散逻辑的出现以不可约的拓扑变形为代价，修正了线性等距假设。任务上下文作为非等距动态算子驱动必要的拓扑扭曲，形成逻辑推理能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) generalize smoothly across continuous semantic spaces, yet strict logical reasoning demands the formation of discrete decision boundaries. Prevailing theories relying on linear isometric projections fail to resolve this fundamental tension. In this work, we argue that task context operates as a non-isometric dynamical operator that enforces a necessary "topological distortion." By applying Gram-Schmidt decomposition to residual-stream activations , we reveal a dual-modulation mechanism driving this process: a class-agnostic topological preservation that anchors global structure to prevent semantic collapse, and a specific algebraic divergence that directionally tears apart cross-class concepts to forge logical boundaries. We validate this geometric evolution across a gradient of tasks, from simple mapping to complex primality testing. Crucially, targeted specific vector ablation establishes a strict causal binding between this topology and model function: algebraically erasing the divergence component collapses parity classification accuracy from 100% to chance levels (38.57%). Furthermore, we uncover a three-phase layer-wise geometric dynamic and demonstrate that under social pressure prompts, models fail to generate sufficient divergence. This results in a "manifold entanglement" that geometrically explains sycophancy and hallucination. Ultimately, our findings revise the linear-isometric presumption, demonstrating that the emergence of discrete logic in LLMs is purchased at an irreducible cost of topological deformation.

</details>


### [38] [AI Generalisation Gap In Comorbid Sleep Disorder Staging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23582)
*Saswata Bose,Suvadeep Maiti,Shivam Kumar Sharma,Mythirayee S,Tapabrata Chakraborti,Srijitesh Rajendran,Raju S. Bapi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了深度学习模型在健康人群上训练的睡眠分期方法无法有效泛化到临床患者（特别是缺血性卒中患者）的问题，通过注意力可视化等技术分析了失败原因，并提出了需要疾病特异性模型的观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确睡眠分期对卒中患者的OSA和低通气诊断至关重要。尽管PSG可靠但成本高、劳动密集且需人工评分。深度学习虽能实现健康人群的自动EEG睡眠分期，但研究发现这些模型在临床人群（睡眠结构紊乱）中泛化能力差，需要系统性研究这一局限性。

Method: 1) 引入iSLEEPS数据集：新标注的缺血性卒中临床数据集（将公开）；2) 使用SE-ResNet加双向LSTM模型进行单通道EEG睡眠分期；3) 采用Grad-CAM注意力可视化分析模型决策；4) 结合临床专家反馈验证可视化结果；5) 进行统计和计算分析比较健康与卒中人群的睡眠结构差异。

Result: 1) 健康与疾病人群间的跨域性能差；2) 注意力可视化显示模型在患者数据中关注生理学上无信息的EEG区域；3) 统计和计算分析证实健康与缺血性卒中人群存在显著睡眠结构差异；4) 临床专家验证了模型关注区域的无意义性。

Conclusion: 基于健康人群训练的睡眠分期模型无法有效应用于临床患者，特别是缺血性卒中人群。需要开发受试者感知或疾病特异性模型，并在部署前进行临床验证。研究强调了临床验证和疾病特异性建模的重要性。

Abstract: Accurate sleep staging is essential for diagnosing OSA and hypopnea in stroke patients. Although PSG is reliable, it is costly, labor-intensive, and manually scored. While deep learning enables automated EEG-based sleep staging in healthy subjects, our analysis shows poor generalization to clinical populations with disrupted sleep. Using Grad-CAM interpretations, we systematically demonstrate this limitation. We introduce iSLEEPS, a newly clinically annotated ischemic stroke dataset (to be publicly released), and evaluate a SE-ResNet plus bidirectional LSTM model for single-channel EEG sleep staging. As expected, cross-domain performance between healthy and diseased subjects is poor. Attention visualizations, supported by clinical expert feedback, show the model focuses on physiologically uninformative EEG regions in patient data. Statistical and computational analyses further confirm significant sleep architecture differences between healthy and ischemic stroke cohorts, highlighting the need for subject-aware or disease-specific models with clinical validation before deployment. A summary of the paper and the code is available at https://himalayansaswatabose.github.io/iSLEEPS_Explainability.github.io/

</details>


### [39] [A Theory of LLM Information Susceptibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23626)
*Zhuo-Yang Song,Hua Xing Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个关于LLM信息敏感性的理论，假设在计算资源足够大的情况下，固定LLM的干预不会增加策略集相对于预算的性能敏感性。该理论通过多变量效用函数框架进行推广，并验证了嵌套协同缩放架构可以超越敏感性界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型越来越多地被部署为智能体系统中的优化模块，但人们对这种LLM介导改进的基本限制仍然知之甚少。需要理解LLM干预何时有效、何时无效，以及如何设计能够实现开放式智能体自我改进的系统架构。

Method: 提出了一个LLM信息敏感性理论，围绕一个核心假设：当计算资源足够大时，固定LLM的干预不会增加策略集相对于预算的性能敏感性。开发了一个多变量效用函数框架，将该假设推广到具有多个协同变化预算通道的架构。讨论了协同缩放可以超越敏感性界限的条件。在结构多样的领域和跨越一个数量级的模型规模上进行了实证验证。
