*Frank Heyen,Michael Sedlmair*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出可视化方法帮助音乐家分析演奏错误和风格变化，当前聚焦于节奏分析并使用MIDI数据


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐家通常依赖听觉分析演奏，但听觉是顺序的，难以快速获取整首或多首录音的概览，需要可视化工具来辅助分析错误和风格变化

Method: 开发多种可视化方法，当前专注于节奏分析，使用MIDI数据简化处理过程

Result: 提出了针对音乐演奏错误和风格变化的可视化分析方案

Conclusion: 可视化方法能够帮助音乐家更有效地分析演奏中的节奏错误和风格变化，弥补单纯依赖听觉的局限性

Abstract: Musicians mostly have to rely on their ears when they want to analyze what they play, for example to detect errors. Since hearing is sequential, it is not possible to quickly grasp an overview over one or multiple recordings of a whole piece of music at once. We therefore propose various visualizations that allow analyzing errors and stylistic variance. Our current approach focuses on rhythm and uses MIDI data for simplicity.

</details>


### [78] [Augmented Reality Visualization for Musical Instrument Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23639)
*Frank Heyen,Michael Sedlmair*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究提出了两种增强现实可视化方案来辅助乐器学习：针对鼓组的投影显示方案和针对吉他的屏幕/AR头显方案，通过案例研究验证了有效性并揭示了设计和技术限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发增强现实可视化工具来辅助乐器学习，特别是针对鼓组和吉他这两种常见乐器，探索不同显示技术（投影、屏幕、AR头显）在乐器教学中的应用潜力。

Method: 1. 为鼓组设计简洁的视觉编码，通过投影仪显示；2. 为吉他设计可视化方案，支持屏幕显示（增强镜像）和光学透视AR头显两种模式；3. 通过案例研究评估原型系统。

Result: 研究证明了所提方案的整体有效性，同时揭示了设计相关和技术限制。投影方案适合鼓组学习，屏幕和AR头显方案为吉他学习提供了不同的信息呈现方式（周围信息和3D内容）。

Conclusion: 增强现实可视化能够有效支持乐器学习，不同显示技术各有优势。投影适合鼓组，屏幕和AR头显为吉他学习提供了灵活的信息呈现方式，但存在设计和技术限制需要进一步解决。

Abstract: We contribute two design studies for augmented reality visualizations that support learning musical instruments. First, we designed simple, glanceable encodings for drum kits, which we display through a projector. As second instrument, we chose guitar and designed visualizations to be displayed either on a screen as an augmented mirror or as an optical see-through AR headset. These modalities allow us to also show information around the instrument and in 3D. We evaluated our prototypes through case studies and our results demonstrate the general effectivity and revealed design-related and technical limitations.

</details>


### [79] [Assessment Design in the AI Era: A Method for Identifying Items Functioning Differentially for Humans and Chatbots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23682)
*Licol Zeinfeld,Alona Strugatski,Ziva Bar-Dov,Ron Blonder,Shelley Rap,Giora Alexandron*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 将心理测量学中的差异项目功能分析应用于评估人类学习者与大型语言模型在测试项目上的系统性表现差异，为AI时代的教育评估设计提供数据驱动的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型在教育中的快速应用，需要开发能够系统评估LLM与人类学习者能力差异的方法，以设计出在AI时代仍然有效、可靠和公平的评估工具。

Method: 结合教育数据挖掘和心理测量理论，采用差异项目功能分析、阴性对照分析和项目-总分相关鉴别分析，比较人类学习者和六个主流聊天机器人在高中化学诊断测试和大学入学考试中的表现差异。

Result: DIF分析方法能够稳健地识别出人类和LLM表现存在系统性差异的项目，揭示了聊天机器人在特定任务维度上的过优或不足表现，为理解AI与人类能力差异提供了实证框架。

Conclusion: DIF分析为理解LLM与人类学习者的能力差异提供了统计上严谨的框架，对改进AI时代的评估设计具有重要价值，能够帮助识别评估工具对AI滥用的脆弱点。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of large language models (LLMs) in education raises profound challenges for assessment design. To adapt assessments to the presence of LLM-based tools, it is crucial to characterize the strengths and weaknesses of LLMs in a generalizable, valid and reliable manner. However, current LLM evaluations often rely on descriptive statistics derived from benchmarks, and little research applies theory-grounded measurement methods to characterize LLM capabilities relative to human learners in ways that directly support assessment design. Here, by combining educational data mining and psychometric theory, we introduce a statistically principled approach for identifying items on which humans and LLMs show systematic response differences, pinpointing where assessments may be most vulnerable to AI misuse, and which task dimensions make problems particularly easy or difficult for generative AI. The method is based on Differential Item Functioning (DIF) analysis -- traditionally used to detect bias across demographic groups -- together with negative control analysis and item-total correlation discrimination analysis. It is evaluated on responses from human learners and six leading chatbots (ChatGPT-4o \& 5.2, Gemini 1.5 \& 3 Pro, Claude 3.5 \& 4.5 Sonnet) to two instruments: a high school chemistry diagnostic test and a university entrance exam. Subject-matter experts then analyzed DIF-flagged items to characterize task dimensions associated with chatbot over- or under-performance. Results show that DIF-informed analytics provide a robust framework for understanding where LLM and human capabilities diverge, and highlight their value for improving the design of valid, reliable, and fair assessment in the AI era.

</details>


### [80] [Exploring Self-Tracking Practices of Older Adults with CVD to Inform the Design of LLM-Enabled Health Data Sensemaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23733)
*Duosi Dai,Pavithren V S Pakianathan,Gunnar Treff,Mahdi Sareban,Jan David Smeddinck,Sanna Kuoppamäki*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探索老年心血管疾病患者如何理解自我追踪数据，并识别LLM支持的设计机会，强调数据解读的情感性、解释性和社会情境性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴设备和移动健康应用在慢性病自我管理中日益普及，但对老年心血管疾病患者来说，数据量过大且难以理解，需要探索他们如何解读这些数据并设计LLM支持的系统来帮助数据理解。

Method: 采用为期七天的日记研究和后续访谈，研究对象为8名年龄在64-82岁之间的心血管疾病患者，通过质性分析识别主题。

Result: 识别出六个主题：1) 应对情感复杂性；2) 拥有健康叙事；3) 优先考虑身体感受；4) 选择性参与健康指标；5) 协商分享的社会技术动态；6) 对AI持谨慎乐观态度。发现自我追踪具有情感性、解释性和社会情境性。

Conclusion: 提出LLM支持的数据理解系统设计方向：支持情感参与、增强患者能动性、承认具身体验、促进临床和社会情境中的对话。为确保安全，需要专家参与机制。这些方向阐明了LLM如何帮助将数据转化为叙事，对人与数据交互和行为改变支持具有重要意义。

Abstract: Wearables and mobile health applications are increasingly adopted for self-management of chronic illnesses; yet the data feels overwhelming for older adults with cardiovascular disease (CVD). This study explores how they make sense of self-tracked data and identifies design opportunities for Large Language Model (LLM)-enabled support. We conducted a seven-day diary study and follow-up interviews with eight CVD patients aged 64-82. We identified six themes: navigating emotional complexity, owning health narratives, prioritizing bodily sensations, selective engagement with health metrics, negotiating socio-technical dynamics of sharing, and cautious optimism toward AI. Findings highlight that self-tracking is affective, interpretive, and socially situated. We outline design directions for LLM-enabled data sensemaking systems: supporting emotional engagement, reinforcing patient agency, acknowledging embodied experiences, and prompting dialogue in clinical and social contexts. To support safety, expert-in-the-loop mechanisms are essential. These directions articulate how LLMs can help translate data into narratives and carry implications for human-data interaction and behavior-change support.

</details>


### [81] [A Reproducible Reality-to-VR Pipeline for Ecologically Valid Aging-in-Place Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23812)
*Ibrahim Bilau,Stacie Smith,Abdurrahman Baru,Marwan Shagar,Brian Jones,Eunhwa Yang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 开发了一个从现实到VR的可重复流水线，用于创建高保真厨房环境模拟，以支持老年人认知老化研究，实现了照片级渲染并保持90Hz帧率，验证了在老年人群中的可行性和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VR用于评估老年人工具性日常生活活动时，生态效度常因环境设计简化或保真度低而受损，无法引发真实临场感。需要开发高保真、可重复的VR环境创建方法，以支持老年人就地养老的认知研究。

Method: 提出可重复的现实到VR流水线：1) 使用地面激光扫描获取亚毫米级几何精度；2) 在Faro SCENE中进行点云处理；3) 在SketchUp中进行几何重拓扑；4) 通过Datasmith集成到Unreal Engine 5，利用Lumen全局光照实现高视觉保真度；5) 实现90Hz稳定帧率以减少老年人群的晕动症风险。

Result: 流水线实现了照片级渲染同时保持90Hz稳定帧率（减少晕动症的关键阈值）。环境支持即时操纵环境变量（如橱柜开关状态切换），提供物理环境无法实现的实验灵活性。17名老年人的参与验证显示晕动症风险最小，且对实验操纵保持敏感性。

Conclusion: 该流水线证明了在老年人就地养老研究中的可行性，为未来比较研究建立了基准。高保真VR环境创建方法能够提高生态效度，同时保持技术可行性和用户安全性，为认知老化研究提供了新的实验工具。

Abstract: Virtual reality (VR) has emerged as a promising tool for assessing instrumental activities of daily living (IADLs) in older adults. However, the ecological validity of these simulations is often compromised by simplified or low-fidelity environmental design that fails to elicit a genuine sense of presence. This paper documents a reproducible Reality-to-VR pipeline for creating a photorealistic environmental simulation to support a study on cognitive aging in place. The proposed workflow captured the as-built kitchen of the Aware Home building at Georgia Tech using Terrestrial Laser Scanning (TLS) for sub-millimeter geometric accuracy, followed by point cloud processing in Faro SCENE, geometric retopology in SketchUp, and integration into Unreal Engine 5 via Datasmith with Lumen global illumination for high visual fidelity. The pipeline achieved photorealistic rendering while maintaining a stable 90 Hz frame rate, a critical threshold for mitigating cybersickness in older populations. The environment also enables instantaneous manipulation of environmental variables, such as switching between closed cabinetry and open shelving, providing experimental flexibility impossible in physical settings. Participant validation with 17 older adults confirmed minimal cybersickness risk and preserved sensitivity to the experimental manipulation, supporting the pipeline's feasibility for aging-in-place research and establishing a benchmark for future comparative studies.

</details>


### [82] [Aesthetics of Robot-Mediated Applied Drama: A Case Study on REMind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23816)
*Elaheh Sanoubari,Alicia Pan,Keith Rebello,Neil Fernandes,Andrew Houston,Kerstin Dautenhahn*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: REMind是一个反欺凌机器人角色扮演游戏，采用机器人媒介应用戏剧方法，通过机器人作为木偶在互动戏剧体验中帮助儿童练习旁观者干预和同伴支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索社交机器人在教育中的新应用模式，超越传统的导师式教学，通过机器人媒介应用戏剧支持反思和社会情感学习，特别是解决反欺凌问题。

Method: 采用机器人媒介应用戏剧方法，将机器人作为逼真木偶融入互动戏剧体验；通过表演艺术专业知识指导设计过程，协调整体体验而非仅依赖机器人表达能力。

Result: 开发了REMind反欺凌机器人角色扮演游戏，展示了如何通过整体体验的协调设计克服当前机器人平台表达能力的限制，创造情感和美学上引人入胜的机器人戏剧。

Conclusion: 机器人戏剧的美学源于整体体验的协调设计，而非仅来自机器人表达能力；机器人媒介应用戏剧为社交机器人教育应用提供了有前景的替代方案。

Abstract: Social robots are increasingly used in education, but most applications cast them as tutors offering explanation-based instruction. We explore an alternative: Robot-Mediated Applied Drama (RMAD), in which robots function as life-like puppets in interactive dramatic experiences designed to support reflection and social-emotional learning. This paper presents REMind, an anti-bullying robot role-play game that helps children rehearse bystander intervention and peer support. We focus on a central design challenge in RMAD: how to make robot drama emotionally and aesthetically engaging despite the limited expressive capacities of current robotic platforms. Through the development of REMind, we show how performing arts expertise informed this process, and argue that the aesthetics of robot drama arise from the coordinated design of the wider experience, not from robot expressivity alone.

</details>


### [83] [CodeExemplar: Example-Based Scaffolding for Introductory Programming in the GenAI Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23830)
*Boxuan Ma,Shinichi Konomi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出基于示例的脚手架方法，使用生成式AI提供与目标任务推理模式匹配但上下文不同的示例，以支持类比迁移并减少抄袭


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI能够轻松生成可运行代码，这在入门编程教学中造成矛盾：学生需要及时帮助，但直接提供解决方案会导致抄袭和推理过程短路

Method: 提出基于示例的脚手架方法，包括二维分类法、设计指南和CodeExemplar原型系统，该系统与自动评分任务集成，通过课堂试点和教师访谈收集初步反馈

Result: 开发了CodeExemplar原型系统，通过课堂试点和教师访谈获得了初步的反馈，验证了基于示例的脚手架方法的可行性

Conclusion: 基于示例的脚手架方法能够平衡生成式AI在编程教育中的帮助作用与防止抄袭的需求，支持学生的类比迁移学习

Abstract: Generative AI (GenAI) can generate working code with minimal effort, creating a tension in introductory programming: students need timely help, yet direct solutions invite copying and can short-circuit reasoning. To address this, we propose example-based scaffolding, where GenAI provides scaffold examples that match a target task's underlying reasoning pattern but differ in contexts to support analogical transfer while reducing copying. We contribute a two-dimensional taxonomy, design guidelines, and CodeExemplar, a prototype integrated with auto-graded tasks, with initial formative feedback from a classroom pilot and instructor interviews.

</details>


### [84] [General Intellectual Humility Is Malleable Through AI-Mediated Reflective Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23855)
*Mohammad Ratul Mahjabin,Raiyan Abdul Baten*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过LLM介导的结构化对话干预（结合认知支架和个性化苏格拉底式反思）能够显著提升一般智力谦逊，效果持久且跨群体普适


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一般智力谦逊（GIH）对推理、学习和社会交往有益，但普遍被认为是一种稳定的、难以干预的特质。本研究旨在挑战这种悲观看法，测试GIH是否可以通过干预得到提升

Method: 采用随机对照实验（N=400），通过LLM介导的结构化对话进行干预。对话从智力谦逊的概念理解开始，逐步进展到应用、分析、评估，并生成与个人相关的新场景。对照组进行时间匹配的活动

Result: 干预组相比对照组：1）系统性地提高了GIH水平；2）降低了等级顺序稳定性；3）将可靠个体改善率提高了三倍。这些效果在两周后的随访中持续存在，没有明显衰减。效果跨政治立场普适，不依赖于基线人格特征

Conclusion: 研究结果挑战了关于GIH可塑性有限的普遍悲观看法，表明通过结构化对话提供的支架式苏格拉底式反思能够产生持久的一般智力谦逊改变

Abstract: General intellectual humility (GIH) -- the recognition that one's beliefs may be fallible and revisable -- is associated with improved reasoning, learning, and social discourse, yet is widely regarded as a stable trait resistant to intervention. We test whether GIH can be elevated through a conversational intervention that combines staged cognitive scaffolding with personalized Socratic reflection. In a randomized controlled experiment (N=400), participants engaged in a structured, LLM-mediated dialogue that progressed from conceptual understanding of intellectual humility to applying, analyzing, evaluating, and generating novel, self-relevant scenarios that instantiate it. Relative to a time-matched control, the intervention produced a systematic increase in GIH, reduced rank-order stability, and tripled the rate of reliable individual improvement. Crucially, these effects persisted over a two-week follow-up without detectable decay. The effects generalized across political affiliation and did not depend on baseline personality profile. These findings challenge the prevailing pessimism regarding the malleability of GIH and suggest that scaffolded, Socratic reflection delivered through structured dialogue can produce durable changes in general intellectual humility.

</details>


### [85] [Skewed Dual Normal Distribution Model: Predicting Touch Pointing Success Rates for Targets Near Screen Edges and Corners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23865)
*Nobuhito Kasahara,Shota Yamanaka,Homei Miyashita*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出偏斜双正态分布模型，用于预测屏幕边缘附近目标的触摸点击成功率，发现目标越接近边缘，点击分布峰值越向边缘偏移，且当目标接触边缘时成功率反而提高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触摸点击成功率预测模型通常排除屏幕边缘附近的目标，但实际设计中经常需要将目标放置在边缘附近，且在可滚动界面中任何元素都可能移动到屏幕边缘。需要研究目标与边缘距离如何影响触摸指向准确性。

Method: 提出偏斜双正态分布模型，假设点击坐标分布受到附近边缘的影响而发生偏斜。通过三个实验（水平指向、垂直指向和二维指向）验证模型的泛化性和实用性。

Result: 结果显示，随着目标接近边缘，点击分布的峰值向边缘偏移，尾部向远离边缘的方向延伸。与先前研究相反，当目标接触边缘时成功率反而提高，表明"将目标与边缘一起点击"的策略有效。模型能够准确预测包括边缘相邻目标在内的各种条件下的成功率。

Conclusion: 偏斜双正态分布模型能够有效预测屏幕边缘附近目标的触摸点击成功率，为界面设计提供了新的理论依据，特别是在需要将目标放置在边缘附近的设计场景中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Typical success-rate prediction models for tapping exclude targets near screen edges. However, design constraints often force such placements, and in scrollable user interfaces, any element can move close to the screen edges. In this work, we model how target-edge distance affects touch pointing accuracy. We propose the Skewed Dual Normal Distribution Model, which assumes the tap-coordinate distribution is skewed by a nearby edge. The results showed that as targets approached the edge, the distribution's peak shifted toward the edge, and its tail extended away. In contrast to prior reports, the success rate improved when the target touched the edge, suggesting a strategy of ``tapping the target together with the edge.'' Our model predicts success rates across a wide range of conditions, including edge-adjacent targets. Through three experiments of horizontal, vertical, and 2D pointing, we demonstrated the generalizability and utility of our proposed model.

</details>


### [86] [Honey, I shrunk the scientist -- Evaluating 2D, 3D, and VR interfaces for navigating samples under the microscope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24337)
*Jan Tiemann,Matthew McGinity,Ulrik Günther*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: VR界面在3D显微镜样本探索中表现最佳，相比2D和3D桌面界面在任务效率、可用性和用户接受度方面均有优势，而3D桌面界面并未超越2D桌面界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在当代生物学和医学中，3D显微镜是广泛使用的成像技术，但在显微镜下导航微米级三维样本（如寻找相关成像区域）对实验者来说是一项繁琐的挑战。研究旨在探索不同界面（2D桌面、3D桌面、虚拟现实）在3D样本探索中的用户体验和性能差异。

Method: 邀请12名熟练的显微镜操作员，在2D、3D和VR三种界面下执行两种不同的探索任务，比较所有条件在速度、可用性和完成度方面的表现。

Result: 结果显示VR在所有场景中均优于2D和3D桌面界面，在任务效率、可用性和用户接受度方面表现出明显优势。有趣的是，虽然VR表现最佳，但3D桌面界面并未超越2D桌面界面。

Conclusion: 虚拟现实界面为3D显微镜样本探索提供了最佳的用户体验和性能，是解决显微镜下三维样本导航挑战的有效方案，而传统的3D桌面界面并未带来预期的改进。

Abstract: In contemporary biology and medicine, 3D microscopy is one of the most widely-used techniques for imaging and manipulation of various kinds of samples. Navigating such a micrometer-sized, 3-dimensional sample under the microscope -- e.g. to find relevant imaging regions -- can pose a tedious challenge for the experimenter. In this paper, we examine whether 2D desktop, 3D desktop, or Virtual Reality (VR) interfaces provide the best user experience and performance for the exploration of 3D samples. We invited 12 skilled microscope operators to perform two different exploration tasks in 2D, 3D and VR and compared all conditions in terms speed, usability, and completion. Our results show a clear benefit when using VR -- in terms of task efficiency, usability, and user acceptance. Intriguingly, while VR outperformed desktop 2D and 3D in all scenarios, 3D desktop did not outperform 2D desktop.

</details>


### [87] [A Neuro-Symbolic System for Interpretable Multimodal Physiological Signals Integration in Human Fatigue Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24358)
*Mohammadreza Jamalifard,Yaxiong Lei,Parasto Azizinezhad,Javier Fumanal-Idocin,Javier Andreu-Perez*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出一种神经符号架构，从眼动追踪和fNIRS信号中学习四个可解释的生理概念，通过注意力编码器和可微近似推理规则进行疲劳分类，实现准确且可解释的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疲劳分类需要既准确又可解释的模型，特别是安全关键应用需要可检查的内部推理。现有方法存在僵化的手工规则和缺乏受试者层面诊断对齐的问题。

Method: 使用注意力编码器从眼动追踪和fNIRS窗口学习四个可解释生理概念（眼动动力学、注视稳定性、前额叶血流动力学、多模态），通过可微近似推理规则结合这些概念，使用学习权重和软阈值，支持受试者特定校准。

Result: 在18名参与者（560个样本）的留一受试者评估中达到72.1%±12.3%准确率，与调优基线相当，同时暴露概念激活和规则触发强度。消融实验显示受试者特定校准提升5.2个百分点，无fNIRS概念下降1.2个百分点，Lukasiewicz算子略优于乘积算子（+0.9 pp）。

Conclusion: 该神经符号架构在保持与基线相当准确性的同时提供了可解释性，概念保真度指标与受试者准确率强相关，为安全关键应用中的疲劳监测提供了可审计的模型。

Abstract: We propose a neuro-symbolic architecture that learns four interpretable physiological concepts, oculomotor dynamics, gaze stability, prefrontal hemodynamics, and multimodal, from eye-tracking and neural hemodynamics, functional near-infrared spectroscopy, (fNIRS) windows using attention-based encoders, and combines them with differentiable approximate reasoning rules using learned weights and soft thresholds, to address both rigid hand-crafted rules and the lack of subject-level alignment diagnostics. We apply this system to fatigue classification from multimodal physiological signals, a domain that requires models that are accurate and interpretable, with internal reasoning that can be inspected for safety-critical use. In leave-one-subject-out evaluation on 18 participants (560 samples), the method achieves 72.1% +/- 12.3% accuracy, comparable to tuned baselines while exposing concept activations and rule firing strengths. Ablations indicate gains from participant-specific calibration (+5.2 pp), a modest drop without the fNIRS concept (-1.2 pp), and slightly better performance with Lukasiewicz operators than product (+0.9 pp). We also introduce concept fidelity, an offline per-subject audit metric from held-out labels, which correlates strongly with per-subject accuracy (r=0.843, p < 0.0001).

</details>


### [88] [Integrating Causal Machine Learning into Clinical Decision Support Systems: Insights from Literature and Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24448)
*Domenique Zipperling,Lukas Schmidt,Benedikt Hahn,Niklas Kühl,Steven Kimbrough*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了如何设计基于因果机器学习的临床决策支持系统，以有效支持临床协作决策，提出了设计需求、原则和特征


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前临床决策支持系统通常基于相关性而非因果关系进行预测。虽然因果机器学习有望通过提供可解释的、治疗特定的推理来改善决策支持，但现有研究多关注模型开发而非面向临床医生的界面设计。需要填补这一设计空白

Method: 采用设计科学研究方法，包括结构化文献综述和访谈经验丰富的医生，从中推导设计需求、原则和特征

Result: 提出了8个基于实证的设计需求、7个设计原则和9个实用设计特征，为设计能够提供因果洞察、无缝集成到临床工作流程、支持信任、可用性和人机协作的CDSS提供了指导

Conclusion: 研究为设计基于因果机器学习的临床决策支持系统提供了系统指导，同时揭示了自动化、责任和监管方面的紧张关系，强调了需要为基于机器学习的医疗产品建立适应性认证流程

Abstract: Current clinical decision support systems (CDSSs) typically base their predictions on correlation, not causation. In recent years, causal machine learning (ML) has emerged as a promising way to improve decision-making with CDSSs by offering interpretable, treatment-specific reasoning. However, existing research often emphasizes model development rather than designing clinician-facing interfaces. To address this gap, we investigated how CDSSs based on causal ML should be designed to effectively support collaborative clinical decision-making. Using a design science research methodology, we conducted a structured literature review and interviewed experienced physicians. From these, we derived eight empirically grounded design requirements, developed seven design principles, and proposed nine practical design features. Our results establish guidance for designing CDSSs that deliver causal insights, integrate seamlessly into clinical workflows, and support trust, usability, and human-AI collaboration. We also reveal tensions around automation, responsibility, and regulation, highlighting the need for an adaptive certification process for ML-based medical products.

</details>


### [89] [Vibe Coding XR: Accelerating AI + XR Prototyping with XR Blocks and Gemini](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24591)
*Ruofei Du,Benjamin Hersh,David Li,Nels Numan,Xun Qian,Yanhe Chen,Zhongyi Zhou,Xingyue Chen,Jiahao Ren,Robert Timothy Bettridge,Steve Toh,David Kim*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: XR Blocks是一个开源模块化WebXR框架，通过高层抽象简化空间计算开发；结合Vibe Coding XR工作流，利用LLM将自然语言意图直接转换为功能性的XR软件，实现快速原型设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型通过"氛围编码"加速了软件开发，但智能扩展现实(XR)体验的原型设计仍然难以实现，主要障碍在于复杂游戏引擎和底层传感器集成的摩擦。需要降低XR开发门槛，让创作者能够快速将想法转化为现实。

Method: 提出XR Blocks框架，将空间计算复杂性抽象为高层、以人为中心的原语；在此基础上开发Vibe Coding XR端到端快速原型工作流，利用LLM将自然语言意图直接转换为功能性XR软件；通过基于Web的界面，创作者可以在1分钟内将高层提示转换为交互式WebXR应用。

Result: 提供了初步技术评估（基于VCXR60数据集）和多样化应用场景展示，包括混合现实真实性、多模态交互和生成式AI集成。代码和实时演示已开源，可通过指定网址访问。

Conclusion: 通过民主化空间软件创建，这项工作使从业者能够绕过底层障碍，快速实现从"想法到现实"的转变，显著降低了XR开发门槛。

Abstract: While large language models have accelerated software development through "vibe coding", prototyping intelligent Extended Reality (XR) experiences remains inaccessible due to the friction of complex game engines and low-level sensor integration. To bridge this gap, we contribute XR Blocks, an open-source, modular WebXR framework that abstracts spatial computing complexities into high-level, human-centered primitives. Building upon this foundation, we present Vibe Coding XR, an end-to-end rapid prototyping workflow that leverages LLMs to translate natural language intent directly into functional XR software. Using a web-based interface, creators can transform high-level prompts (e.g., "create a dandelion that reacts to hand") into interactive WebXR applications in under a minute. We provide a preliminary technical evaluation on a pilot dataset (VCXR60) alongside diverse application scenarios highlighting mixed-reality realism, multi-modal interaction, and generative AI integrations. By democratizing spatial software creation, this work empowers practitioners to bypass low-level hurdles and rapidly move from "idea to reality." Code and live demos are available at https://xrblocks.github.io/gem and https://github.com/google/xrblocks.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [90] [The HyperFrog Cryptosystem: High-Genus Voxel Topology as a Trapdoor for Post-Quantum KEMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23505)
*Victor Duarte Melo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: HyperFrog是一种实验性后量子密钥封装机制，探索LWE变体设计空间，其中密钥不是从独立乘积分布采样，而是从离散拓扑结构确定性派生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索LWE设计空间的变体，研究从拓扑结构派生密钥而非传统随机采样的方法，以了解拓扑派生密钥如何与已知格攻击和译码攻击相互作用。

Method: 在三维中嵌入体素网格，使用拓扑挖掘程序搜索具有规定复杂度（通过环数测量）的连通子图，将结果结构编码为稀疏二进制密钥向量，采用Fujisaki-Okamoto变换实现IND-CCA安全性。

Result: 提出了完整的构造、参数化和序列化格式，提供了包含自测试和基准测试的参考实现，分析了拓扑派生密钥与已知攻击的相互作用，并确定了保守参数选择和完整安全分析所需的开放问题。

Conclusion: HyperFrog是一个研究工具而非生产就绪的KEM，展示了从拓扑结构派生LWE密钥的可行性，为后量子密码学提供了新的设计探索方向。

Abstract: HyperFrog is an experimental post-quantum Key Encapsulation Mechanism that explores a variant of the Learning With Errors (LWE) design space in which the secret is not sampled from an independent product distribution, but is deterministically derived from discrete topological structure. The scheme embeds a voxel grid in three dimensions and uses a topology mining procedure to search for connected subgraphs with prescribed complexity, measured by cyclomatic number (high genus). The resulting structure is encoded as a sparse binary secret vector, inducing strong geometric constraints on the secret distribution while retaining a large combinatorial search space. Encapsulation produces noisy linear relations over public parameters and derives the shared key via hashing; a Fujisaki-Okamoto style transform is used to target IND-CCA security in the random oracle model. We present the construction, parameterization, and serialization format, together with a reference implementation featuring self-tests and benchmarking on commodity CPUs. We also discuss how topology-derived secrets interact with known lattice and decoding attacks, and we outline open problems required for conservative parameter selection and for a full security analysis. HyperFrog is intended as a research vehicle rather than a production-ready KEM.

</details>


### [91] [n-VM: A Multi-VM Layer-1 Architecture with Shared Identity and Token State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23670)
*Jian Sheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出n-VM架构，一个支持n个异构虚拟机作为平等执行环境的Layer-1区块链系统，通过共享共识和状态解决多链生态的身份碎片化、流动性割裂和桥依赖问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多链生态系统中存在的三个核心问题：1) 身份碎片化（用户在不同链上需要不同身份），2) 流动性割裂（资产分散在不同链上），3) 桥依赖的跨链转账（需要依赖外部桥接器）。这些问题导致用户体验复杂、安全风险增加和效率低下。

Method: 设计包含三个核心组件的n-VM架构：1) 基于操作码前缀的路由调度器，2) 统一身份层（32字节承诺锚定各VM特定地址），3) 统一代币账本（在通用余额存储上暴露ERC-20、SPL等VM原生接口）。通过形式化路由、身份派生和代币转移语义，证明跨VM转移的原子性和身份隔离性。具体实例化包含五个虚拟机：原生运行时、EVM、SVM、比特币脚本和TVM，并引入基于上下文的分片和写集调度器实现并行执行。

Result: 在标准密码学假设下证明了跨VM转移的原子性和身份隔离性。通过分析性吞吐量模型预测，在商用硬件上可实现约16,000到66,000 TPS的交易处理能力。具体实例化展示了五个异构VM的协同工作能力。

Conclusion: n-VM架构为多链生态系统提供了一种统一的Layer-1解决方案，通过共享共识和状态同时支持多个异构虚拟机，有效解决了身份碎片化、流动性割裂和桥依赖问题，同时保持了高性能和可扩展性。

Abstract: Multi-chain ecosystems suffer from fragmented identity, siloed liquidity, and bridge-dependent token transfers. We present n-VM, a Layer-1 architecture that hosts n heterogeneous virtual machines as co-equal execution environments over shared consensus and shared state. The design combines three components: a dispatcher that routes transactions by opcode prefix, a unified identity layer in which one 32-byte commitment anchors VM-specifific addresses, and a unified token ledger that exposes VM-native interfaces such as ERC-20 and SPL over a common balance store. We formalize routing, identity derivation, and token transfer semantics, and prove cross-VM transfer atomicity and identity isolation under standard cryptographic assumptions. We describe a concrete instantiation with five VMs: a native runtime, EVM, SVM, Bitcoin Script, and TVM. We also present context-based sharding and a write-set scheduler for parallel execution. Under an analytical throughput model, the architecture admits a projected range of about 16,000 to 66,000 transactions per second on commodity hardware.

</details>


### [92] [Leveraging Large Language Models for Trustworthiness Assessment of Web Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23781)
*Oleksandr Yarotskyi,José D'Abruzzo Pereira,João R. Campos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种利用大语言模型自动化评估Web应用可信度的方法，通过验证安全编码实践的遵循情况，并扩展基于逻辑偏好分数的分层质量模型来计算整体可信度分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web应用安全评估面临挑战：现有技术主要关注已知漏洞检测或依赖人工评估，缺乏可扩展性；安全编码实践的识别和验证主要依赖人工专家评审，耗时、主观且难以扩展。需要自动化、可扩展的信任评估方法。

Method: 1. 利用大语言模型验证安全编码实践的遵循情况；2. 比较五种最先进LLM的提示工程技术，包括零样本分类、语义定义增强、调用图结构上下文和显式指令指导；3. 扩展基于逻辑偏好分数的分层质量模型，使用LLM输出填充质量属性并计算整体可信度分数。

Result: 实验结果表明：过多的结构上下文会引入噪声，而基于规则的指令提示能提高评估可靠性；生成的可信度分数能够区分安全实现和易受攻击实现，支持使用LLM进行可扩展、上下文感知的信任评估的可行性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了利用大语言模型自动化评估Web应用可信度的可行性，通过验证安全编码实践和计算整体可信度分数，为可扩展、上下文感知的信任评估提供了实用方法。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of web applications has made their security a critical concern and has increased the need for systematic ways to assess whether they can be considered trustworthy. However, "trust" assessment remains an open problem as existing techniques primarily focus on detecting known vulnerabilities or depend on manual evaluation, which limits their scalability; therefore, evaluating adherence to secure coding practices offers a complementary, pragmatic perspective by focusing on observable development behaviors. In practice, the identification and verification of secure coding practices are predominantly performed manually, relying on expert knowledge and code reviews, which is time-consuming, subjective, and difficult to scale. This study presents an empirical methodology to automate the trustworthiness assessment of web applications by leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) to verify adherence to secure coding practices. We conduct a comparative analysis of prompt engineering techniques across five state-of-the-art LLMs, ranging from baseline zero-shot classification to prompts enriched with semantic definitions, structural context derived from call graphs, and explicit instructional guidance. Furthermore, we propose an extension of a hierarchical Quality Model (QM) based on the Logic Score of Preference (LSP), in which LLM outputs are used to populate the model's quality attributes and compute a holistic trustworthiness score. Experimental results indicate that excessive structural context can introduce noise, whereas rule-based instructional prompting improves assessment reliability. The resulting trustworthiness score allows discriminating between secure and vulnerable implementations, supporting the feasibility of using LLMs for scalable and context-aware trust assessment.

</details>


### [93] [How Vulnerable Are Edge LLMs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23822)
*Ao Ding,Hongzong Li,Zi Liang,Zhanpeng Shi,Shuxin Zhuang,Shiqin Tang,Rong Feng,Ping Lu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 量化部署在边缘设备上的大语言模型仍面临查询式知识提取风险，CLIQ框架能有效恢复模型语义知识


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在计算和量化约束下部署在边缘设备上的大语言模型的安全隐患，特别是量化是否真的能有效防止知识提取攻击

Method: 提出CLIQ（聚类指令查询）框架，通过结构化查询构建提高语义覆盖率并减少冗余，在有限查询预算下更有效地提取量化模型的语义知识

Result: 在量化Qwen模型（INT8/INT4）上的实验表明，CLIQ在BERTScore、BLEU和ROUGE指标上始终优于原始查询，能在有限预算下实现更高效的知识提取

Conclusion: 量化本身不能有效防止查询式知识提取攻击，揭示了边缘部署LLM中一个先前未被充分探索的安全风险

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed on edge devices under strict computation and quantization constraints, yet their security implications remain unclear. We study query-based knowledge extraction from quantized edge-deployed LLMs under realistic query budgets and show that, although quantization introduces noise, it does not remove the underlying semantic knowledge, allowing substantial behavioral recovery through carefully designed queries. To systematically analyze this risk, we propose \textbf{CLIQ} (\textbf{Cl}ustered \textbf{I}nstruction \textbf{Q}uerying), a structured query construction framework that improves semantic coverage while reducing redundancy. Experiments on quantized Qwen models (INT8/INT4) demonstrate that CLIQ consistently outperforms original queries across BERTScore, BLEU, and ROUGE, enabling more efficient extraction under limited budgets. These results indicate that quantization alone does not provide effective protection against query-based extraction, highlighting a previously underexplored security risk in edge-deployed LLMs.

</details>


### [94] [An Empirical Analysis of Google Play Data Safety Disclosures: A Consistency Study of Privacy Indicators in Mobile Gaming Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23935)
*Bakheet Aljedaani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 实证分析41款移动游戏应用，比较Google Play数据安全标签与APK静态分析提取的隐私指标一致性，发现设备ID披露一致性较高(87.8%)，但位置相关披露不一致率最高(56.1%)，儿童与普通应用模式相似，差异无统计学意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Google Play数据安全部分依赖开发者自我报告，其披露准确性存在担忧。本研究旨在调查开发者报告的数据安全披露与从APK提取的可观察隐私指标之间的一致性，评估当前市场透明度机制的可靠性。

Method: 采用静态分析方法从41款移动游戏应用的APK文件中提取关键隐私指标（设备ID、数据共享、个人信息访问、位置访问），使用结构化一致性评估框架系统比较这些指标与Google Play数据安全标签中的相应披露，并进行卡方统计检验。

Result: 不同隐私类别一致性水平各异：设备ID披露一致性相对较高(87.8%)，其他指标存在显著不匹配。位置相关披露不一致率最高(56.1%)，其次是个人信息和数据共享。儿童导向应用与普通受众应用显示相似的不匹配模式，卡方检验表明差异无统计学意义，表明披露不一致与应用类别无关，而是反映更广泛的生态系统层面挑战。

Conclusion: 当前市场透明度机制在可靠性方面存在局限性，需要改进验证和核实方法，以确保移动应用生态系统中准确的隐私报告。披露不一致是生态系统层面的普遍问题，而非特定应用类别特征。

Abstract: The Google Play marketplace has introduced the Data Safety section to improve transparency regarding how mobile applications (apps) collect, share, and protect user data. This mechanism requires developers to disclose privacy and security-related practices. However, the reliability of these disclosures remains dependent on developer self-reporting, raising concerns about their accuracy. This study investigates the consistency between developer-reported Data Safety disclosures and observable privacy indicators extracted from Android Application Packages (APKs). An empirical analysis was conducted on a dataset of 41 mobile gaming apps. A static analysis approach was used to extract key privacy indicators from APK files, including device IDs, data sharing, personal information access, and location access. These indicators were systematically compared with the corresponding disclosures reported in the Google Play Data Safety labels using a structured consistency evaluation framework. The results revealed varying levels of agreement across privacy categories. Device ID disclosures demonstrated relatively high consistency (87.8%), whereas other indicators exhibited substantial mismatches. Location-related disclosures showed the highest inconsistency rate (56.1%), followed by personal information and data sharing. Comparative analysis between children-oriented and general-audience apps revealed similar mismatch patterns. Also, Chi-square statistical tests indicate that these differences are not statistically significant, suggesting that disclosure inconsistencies are not associated with app category but instead reflect broader ecosystem-level challenges. These findings highlight limitations in the reliability of current marketplace transparency mechanisms and emphasize the need for improved validation and verification approaches to ensure accurate privacy reporting in mobile app ecosystems.

</details>


### [95] [Walma: Learning to See Memory Corruption in WebAssembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24167)
*Oussama Draissi,Mark Günzel,Ahmad-Reza Sadeghi,Lucas Davi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Walma是一个基于机器学习的WebAssembly线性内存认证框架，通过分类内存快照来检测内存损坏和外部篡改攻击，在真实CVE应用中验证了CNN分类的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WebAssembly的单片线性内存模型容易受到内存损坏攻击，可能导致浏览器中的跨站脚本攻击，或在恶意主机篡改模块状态时不被检测。现有防御方案依赖于侵入式二进制插桩或自定义运行时，且未解决对抗性主机模型下的运行时完整性验证问题。

Method: 提出Walma框架，利用机器学习对内存快照进行分类来检测内存损坏和外部篡改。在六个真实CVE影响的应用上评估了三个验证后端（cpu-wasm、cpu-tch、gpu）和三种插桩策略。

Result: CNN-based分类能有效检测具有结构化内存布局的应用中的内存损坏。粗粒度边界检查开销低至1.07倍，细粒度监控引入较高（1.5-1.8倍）但可预测的成本。评估量化了不同部署配置下的准确性和开销权衡。

Conclusion: 基于机器学习的内存认证对于WebAssembly具有实际可行性，Walma框架为检测内存损坏和对抗性主机篡改提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: WebAssembly's (Wasm) monolithic linear memory model facilitates memory corruption attacks that can escalate to cross-site scripting in browsers or go undetected when a malicious host tampers with a module's state. Existing defenses rely on invasive binary instrumentation or custom runtimes, and do not address runtime integrity verification under an adversarial host model. We present Walma, a framework for WebAssembly Linear Memory Attestation that leverages machine learning to detect memory corruption and external tampering by classifying memory snapshots. We evaluate Walma on six real-world CVE-affected applications across three verification backends (cpu-wasm, cpu-tch, gpu) and three instrumentation policies. Our results demonstrate that CNN-based classification can effectively detect memory corruption in applications with structured memory layouts, with coarse-grained boundary checks incurring as low as 1.07x overhead, while fine-grained monitoring introduces higher (1.5x--1.8x) but predictable costs. Our evaluation quantifies the accuracy and overhead trade-offs across deployment configurations, demonstrating the practical feasibility of ML-based memory attestation for WebAssembly.

</details>


### [96] [Towards Remote Attestation of Microarchitectural Attacks: The Case of Rowhammer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24172)
*Martin Herrmann,Oussama Draissi,Christian Niesler,Lucas Davi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: HammerWatch：首个面向Rowhammer攻击的微架构感知远程证明协议，通过检测硬件诱导的干扰行为而非预防攻击，利用ECC DRAM的机器检查异常和PRAC计数器作为证据，使用TPM锚定的哈希链保护测量结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微架构漏洞日益破坏硬件作为可信根的基本假设，预防机制往往滞后于攻击技术的发展，导致已部署系统无法持续保持可信。需要从预防转向检测，通过微架构感知的远程证明来评估系统硬件安全性。

Method: 提出HammerWatch协议作为微架构感知远程证明的首个实例化实现。利用商用平台可用的内存级证据：1) ECC DRAM的机器检查异常(MCEs)；2) 每行激活计数(PRAC)的计数器指标。使用TPM锚定的哈希链保护这些测量结果免受内核级攻击者篡改。

Result: 在商用硬件上实现HammerWatch，并在20000个模拟的良性/恶意访问模式上进行评估。结果表明，在保守启发式下，验证者能可靠地区分Rowhammer类行为与良性操作，检测准确率达到预期目标。

Conclusion: 检测导向的远程证明是可行的，能够补充不完整的预防机制。HammerWatch展示了微架构感知远程证明的实用性，为硬件可信评估提供了新范式，从预防转向检测的转变具有实际可行性。

Abstract: Microarchitectural vulnerabilities increasingly undermine the assumption that hardware can be treated as a reliable root of trust. Prevention mechanisms often lag behind evolving attack techniques, leaving deployed systems unable to assume continued trustworthiness. We propose a shift from prevention to detection through microarchitectural-aware remote attestation. As a first instantiation of this idea, we present HammerWatch, a Rowhammer-aware remote attestation protocol that enables an external verifier to assess whether a system exhibits hardware-induced disturbance behavior. HammerWatch leverages memory-level evidence available on commodity platforms, specifically Machine-Check Exceptions (MCEs) from ECC DRAM and counter-based indicators from Per-Row Activation Counting (PRAC), and protects these measurements against kernel-level adversaries using TPM-anchored hash chains. We implement HammerWatch on commodity hardware and evaluate it on 20000 simulated benign and malicious access patterns. Our results show that the verifier reliably distinguishes Rowhammer-like behavior from benign operation under conservative heuristics, demonstrating that detection-oriented attestation is feasible and can complement incomplete prevention mechanisms

</details>


### [97] [Analysing the Safety Pitfalls of Steering Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24543)
*Yuxiao Li,Alina Fastowski,Efstratios Zaradoukas,Bardh Prenkaj,Gjergji Kasneci*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 激活引导技术可显著影响LLM越狱攻击成功率，最高可增加57%或降低50%，揭示了可控性与安全性之间的权衡


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导技术已被证明能有效塑造LLM行为而无需权重更新，但其安全影响尚未得到充分探索。本研究旨在系统评估对比激活加法（CAA）这一广泛使用的引导方法在统一评估协议下的安全影响。

Method: 使用JailbreakBench作为基准，系统评估对比激活加法（CAA）获得的引导向量对越狱攻击成功率的影响。在不同LLM家族和规模上进行实验，分析引导向量对攻击成功率的影响模式。

Result: 引导向量能显著影响越狱攻击成功率：在简单模板攻击下放大效应更强；根据目标行为方向，攻击成功率可大幅增加（最高57%）或降低（最高50%）。这种现象归因于引导向量与拒绝行为的潜在方向之间的重叠。

Conclusion: 激活引导技术在LLM中引入了先前未观察到的安全漏洞，揭示了可控性与安全性之间的根本权衡。研究为这一发现提供了可追溯的解释，强调了在应用引导技术时需要考虑的安全风险。

Abstract: Activation steering has emerged as a powerful tool to shape LLM behavior without the need for weight updates. While its inherent brittleness and unreliability are well-documented, its safety implications remain underexplored. In this work, we present a systematic safety audit of steering vectors obtained with Contrastive Activation Addition (CAA), a widely used steering approach, under a unified evaluation protocol. Using JailbreakBench as benchmark, we show that steering vectors consistently influence the success rate of jailbreak attacks, with stronger amplification under simple template-based attacks. Across LLM families and sizes, steering the model in specific directions can drastically increase (up to 57%) or decrease (up to 50%) its attack success rate (ASR), depending on the targeted behavior. We attribute this phenomenon to the overlap between the steering vectors and the latent directions of refusal behavior. Thus, we offer a traceable explanation for this discovery. Together, our findings reveal the previously unobserved origin of this safety gap in LLMs, highlighting a trade-off between controllability and safety.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [98] [optimade-maker: Automated generation of interoperable materials APIs from static data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23536)
*Kristjan Eimre,Matthew L. Evans,Bud Macaulay,Xing Wang,Jusong Yu,Nicola Marzari,Gian-Marco Rignanese,Giovanni Pizzi*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: OPTIMADE-maker：用于从原始原子结构数据自动生成OPTIMADE兼容API的轻量级工具包，降低材料数据互操作性发布的技术门槛


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料科学、凝聚态物理和化学领域的原子结构数据日益数字化，但异构数据源的互操作访问存在技术障碍。OPTIMADE规范定义了标准REST API，但部署和维护符合规范的服务对许多数据提供者来说技术门槛较高

Method: 开发optimade-maker工具包，支持从原始原子结构和性质数据自动生成OPTIMADE兼容API。工具包支持多种原始数据集，实现标准化OPTIMADE数据表示，并允许在本地和生产环境中快速部署API

Result: 在Materials Cloud Archive上实现了自动化服务，自动为贡献的数据集创建和发布OPTIMADE API，实现即时可发现性和互操作性。同时为剑桥结构数据库(CSD)和无机晶体结构数据库(ICSD)实现了数据转换管道，通过OPTIMADE框架提供统一访问

Conclusion: optimade-maker通过降低互操作数据发布的技术门槛，向可扩展、FAIR原则的材料数据生态系统迈出了重要一步，整合了社区贡献和策划的数据库

Abstract: Atomistic structural data are central to materials science, condensed matter physics, and chemistry, and are increasingly digitised across diverse repositories and databases. Interoperable access to these heterogeneous data sources enables reusable clients and tools, and is essential for cross-database analyses and data-driven materials discovery. Toward this aim, the OPTIMADE (Open Databases Integration for Materials Design) specification defines a standard REST API for atomistic structures and related properties. However, deploying and maintaining compliant services remains technically demanding and poses a significant barrier for many data providers. Here, we present optimade-maker, a lightweight toolkit for the automated generation of OPTIMADE-compliant APIs directly from raw atomistic structure and property data. The toolkit supports a wide range of raw datasets, enables conversion to a standardised OPTIMADE data representation, and allows for rapid deployment of APIs in both local and production environments. We further demonstrate it through an automated service on the Materials Cloud Archive, which automatically creates and publishes OPTIMADE APIs for contributed datasets, enabling immediate discoverability and interoperability. In addition, we implement data transformation pipelines for the Cambridge Structural Database (CSD) and the Inorganic Crystal Structure Database (ICSD), enabling unified access to these curated resources through the OPTIMADE framework. By lowering the technical barriers to interoperable data publication, optimade-maker represents an important step toward a scalable, FAIR materials data ecosystem integrating both community-contributed and curated databases.

</details>


### [99] [An In-Depth Study of Filter-Agnostic Vector Search on a PostgreSQL Database System: [Experiments and Analysis]](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23710)
*Duo Lu,Helena Caminal,Manos Chatzakis,Yannis Papakonstantinou,Yannis Chronis,Vaibhav Jain,Fatma Özcan*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该论文首次在生产级PostgreSQL兼容系统中深入分析过滤无关的向量搜索算法，发现系统级开销（页面访问、数据检索）而非仅距离计算成本决定算法选择，图索引方法在真实数据库环境中可能失去理论优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常在专用库中评估向量搜索算法，做出过于乐观的假设，与生产级数据库系统的实际情况不符。需要在实际数据库环境中重新评估过滤无关向量搜索算法的性能特征和权衡。

Method: 在生产级PostgreSQL兼容系统中首次对过滤无关向量搜索算法进行深入分析，系统评估后过滤和内联过滤策略，覆盖广泛的选择性和相关性范围，比较图索引（如NaviX/ACORN）和聚类索引（如ScaNN）的性能。

Result: 发现最优算法选择不仅取决于距离计算成本，系统级开销（包括距离计算和过滤操作中的页面访问、数据检索）起关键作用。图索引方法可能产生过多的过滤检查和系统开销，抵消其在理论上的优势，而聚类索引在真实数据库环境中表现更优。

Conclusion: 过滤无关向量搜索算法的最优选择不是绝对的，而是需要系统感知的决策，取决于工作负载特征与底层数据访问成本的相互作用。研究为数据库社区提供了关键见解和实用指南。

Abstract: Filtered Vector Search (FVS) is critical for supporting semantic search and GenAI applications in modern database systems. However, existing research most often evaluates algorithms in specialized libraries, making optimistic assumptions that do not align with enterprise-grade database systems. Our work challenges this premise by demonstrating that in a production-grade database system, commonly made assumptions do not hold, leading to performance characteristics and algorithmic trade-offs that are fundamentally different from those observed in isolated library settings. This paper presents the first in-depth analysis of filter-agnostic FVS algorithms within a production PostgreSQL-compatible system. We systematically evaluate post-filtering and inline-filtering strategies across a wide range of selectivities and correlations.
  Our central finding is that the optimal algorithm is not dictated by the cost of distance computations alone, but that system-level overheads that come from both distance computations and filter operations (like page accesses and data retrieval) play a significant role. We demonstrate that graph-based approaches (such as NaviX/ACORN) can incur prohibitive numbers of filter checks and system-level overheads, compared with clustering-based indexes such as ScaNN, often canceling out their theoretical benefits in real-world database environments.
  Ultimately, our findings provide the database community with crucial insights and practical guidelines, demonstrating that the optimal choice for a filter-agnostic FVS algorithm is not absolute, but rather a system-aware decision contingent on the interplay between workload characteristics and the underlying costs of data access in a real-world database architecture.

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [A formalization of System I with type Top in Agda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23652)
*Agustín Séttimo,Cristian Sottile,Cecilia Manzino*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 在System I（一种将同构类型视为相等的简单类型λ演算）基础上，添加Top类型并形式化验证其性质


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展System I演算的功能，使其包含Top类型（所有类型的超类型），并建立完整的形式化验证框架以确保该扩展系统的可靠性

Method: 提出System I的变体，添加Top类型构造；使用Agda定理证明器进行完整形式化，包括类型系统定义、求值规则，并证明进展定理和强规范化定理

Result: 成功实现了包含Top类型的System I变体的Agda形式化，完整证明了进展定理（well-typed项不会卡住）和强规范化定理（所有well-typed项都会终止）

Conclusion: System I演算可以成功扩展Top类型，并通过形式化验证确保了该扩展系统的类型安全性和计算可靠性，为类型同构理论的实际应用提供了更丰富的类型系统基础

Abstract: System I is a recently introduced simply-typed lambda calculus with pairs where isomorphic types are considered equal. In this work we propose a variant of System I with the type Top, and present a complete formalization of this calculus in Agda, which includes the proofs of progress and strong normalization.

</details>


### [101] [Hybrid Spatiotemporal Logic for Automotive Applications: Modeling and Model-Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24443)
*Radu-Florin Tulcan,Rose Bohrer,Yoàv Montacute,Kevin Zhou,Yusuke Kawamoto,Ichiro Hasuo*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 提出用于高速公路驾驶安全的混合时空逻辑HSTL，包含基线模型检测算法和两种优化算法，在常见驾驶场景中验证并实现指数级性能提升


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汽车安全应用需要能够同时处理空间和时间约束的形式化规范语言，传统时空逻辑缺乏对单个车辆及其历史位置的精确引用能力

Method: 定义混合时空逻辑HSTL语义，提供基线模型检测算法，提出两种基于可达状态和状态间可能转移的搜索空间优化算法

Result: 在安全跟车、安全交叉、超车和编队等常见驾驶场景中评估三种算法，优化算法相比基线算法实现指数级性能提升

Conclusion: HSTL为汽车安全应用提供了强大的形式化规范语言，优化的模型检测算法显著提高了验证效率，适用于复杂驾驶场景分析

Abstract: We introduce a hybrid spatiotemporal logic for automotive safety applications (HSTL), focused on highway driving. Spatiotemporal logic features specifications about vehicles throughout space and time, while hybrid logic enables precise references to individual vehicles and their historical positions. We define the semantics of HSTL and provide a baseline model-checking algorithm for it. We propose two optimized model-checking algorithms, which reduce the search space based on the reachable states and possible transitions from one state to another. All three model-checking algorithms are evaluated on a series of common driving scenarios such as safe following, safe crossings, overtaking, and platooning. An exponential performance improvement is observed for the optimized algorithms.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [Human-in-the-Loop Pareto Optimization: Trade-off Characterization for Assist-as-Needed Training and Performance Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23777)
*Harun Tolasa,Volkan Patoglu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种人机交互帕累托优化方法，用于量化运动学习任务中任务难度与用户表现之间的权衡关系，并展示其在辅助训练协议设计、个体训练进展评估和用户间公平比较中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在运动技能训练和物理康复中，任务难度与用户表现之间存在固有权衡。量化这种权衡对于评估用户表现、设计按需辅助协议以及评估训练协议效果至关重要。现有方法难以系统地表征这种权衡关系。

Method: 提出人机交互帕累托优化方法，采用贝叶斯多目标优化系统高效地进行帕累托表征。使用混合模型：性能用定量指标衡量，感知挑战水平用定性指标捕捉。通过用户研究验证可行性，并在具有触觉反馈的手动技能训练任务中展示三个应用案例。

Result: 成功展示了HiL帕累托表征的可行性。三个应用案例表明：1）可用于设计按需辅助训练协议并评估其相对于基线自适应辅助协议的效果；2）支持在不同辅助水平下公平评估个体训练进展，即使在没有辅助时用户无法完成任务；3）能够实现不同用户间的公平性能比较，因为该方法捕捉了每个用户在可行辅助水平下的最佳可能表现。

Conclusion: 提出的HiL帕累托优化方法能够有效表征运动学习任务中难度与表现的权衡关系，为按需辅助协议设计、个体训练进展评估和跨用户公平比较提供了系统框架，具有重要的理论和应用价值。

Abstract: During human motor skill training and physical rehabilitation, there is an inherent trade-off between task difficulty and user performance. Characterizing this trade-off is crucial for evaluating user performance, designing assist-as-needed (AAN) protocols, and assessing the efficacy of training protocols. In this study, we propose a novel human-in-the-loop (HiL) Pareto optimization approach to characterize the trade-off between task performance and the perceived challenge level of motor learning or rehabilitation tasks. We adapt Bayesian multi-criteria optimization to systematically and efficiently perform HiL Pareto characterizations. Our HiL optimization employs a hybrid model that measures performance with a quantitative metric, while the perceived challenge level is captured with a qualitative metric. We demonstrate the feasibility of the proposed HiL Pareto characterization through a user study. Furthermore, we present the utility of the framework through three use cases in the context of a manual skill training task with haptic feedback. First, we demonstrate how the characterized trade-off can be used to design a sample AAN training protocol for a motor learning task and to evaluate the group-level efficacy of the proposed AAN protocol relative to a baseline adaptive assistance protocol. Second, we demonstrate that individual-level comparisons of the trade-offs characterized before and after the training session enable fair evaluation of training progress under different assistance levels. This evaluation method is more general than standard performance evaluations, as it can provide insights even when users cannot perform the task without assistance. Third, we show that the characterized trade-offs also enable fair performance comparisons among different users, as they capture the best possible performance of each user under all feasible assistance levels.

</details>


### [103] [Quadrature Oscillation System for Coordinated Motion in Crawling Origami Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23666)
*Sean Liu,Ankur Mehta,Wenzhong Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发了一种四相信号振荡器系统，用于无电子器件的折纸机器人控制，实现了更复杂的协调运动


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无电子器件的折纸机器人能在极端环境下工作，但缺乏复杂控制系统限制了其自主行为。需要开发更先进的振荡器系统来实现更复杂的控制功能。

Method: 开发了一种四相信号振荡系统，能生成四个四分之一周期相位差的方波信号，实现四种不同状态。将此系统应用于折纸爬行机器人设计中，实现协调运动。

Result: 成功设计并构建了使用四相振荡器的折纸爬行机器人，实现了协调的爬行运动。该系统展示了无电子器件折纸机器人实现更复杂控制功能的潜力。

Conclusion: 四相振荡系统为无电子器件的折纸机器人提供了更复杂的控制能力，为具有先进自主行为的快速设计折纸机器人开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Origami-inspired robots offer rapid, accessible design and manufacture with diverse functionalities. In particular, origami robots without conventional electronics have the unique advantage of functioning in extreme environments such as ones with high radiation or large magnetic fields. However, the absence of sophisticated control systems limits these robots to simple autonomous behaviors. In our previous studies, we developed a printable, electronics-free, and self-sustained oscillator that generates simple complementary square-wave signals. Our study presents a quadrature oscillation system capable of generating four square-wave signals a quarter-cycle out of phase, enabling four distinct states. Such control signals are important in various engineering and robotics applications, such as orchestrating limb movements in bio-inspired robots. We demonstrate the practicality and value of this oscillation system by designing and constructing an origami crawling robot that utilizes the quadrature oscillator to achieve coordinated locomotion. Together, the oscillator and robot illustrate the potential for more complex control and functions in origami robotics, paving the way for more electronics-free, rapid-design origami robots with advanced autonomous behaviors.

</details>


### [104] [SafeFlow: Real-Time Text-Driven Humanoid Whole-Body Control via Physics-Guided Rectified Flow and Selective Safety Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23983)
*Hanbyel Cho,Sang-Hun Kim,Jeonguk Kang,Donghan Koo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SafeFlow：结合物理引导运动生成与3阶段安全门控的文本驱动人形机器人全身控制框架，解决运动轨迹物理不可行问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仅基于运动学的文本驱动运动生成方法存在物理幻觉问题，产生的运动轨迹在物理上不可行，难以被下游运动跟踪控制器执行，且在分布外用户输入下问题更严重，缺乏明确的物理感知目标

Method: 采用两级架构：高层使用VAE潜在空间中的物理引导整流流匹配生成运动轨迹，并通过Reflow加速采样；3阶段安全门控包括：基于马氏距离的语义OOD提示检测、通过方向敏感性差异度量的不稳定生成过滤、以及关节和速度限制等硬运动学约束

Result: 在Unitree G1上的实验表明，SafeFlow在成功率、物理合规性和推理速度方面优于先前的基于扩散的方法，同时保持了多样化的表达能力

Conclusion: SafeFlow通过结合物理引导的运动生成和分层安全机制，有效解决了文本驱动人形机器人控制中的物理可行性和安全问题，实现了更可靠的实际部署

Abstract: Recent advances in real-time interactive text-driven motion generation have enabled humanoids to perform diverse behaviors. However, kinematics-only generators often exhibit physical hallucinations, producing motion trajectories that are physically infeasible to track with a downstream motion tracking controller or unsafe for real-world deployment. These failures often arise from the lack of explicit physics-aware objectives for real-robot execution and become more severe under out-of-distribution (OOD) user inputs. Hence, we propose SafeFlow, a text-driven humanoid whole-body control framework that combines physics-guided motion generation with a 3-Stage Safety Gate driven by explicit risk indicators. SafeFlow adopts a two-level architecture. At the high level, we generate motion trajectories using Physics-Guided Rectified Flow Matching in a VAE latent space to improve real-robot executability, and further accelerate sampling via Reflow to reduce the number of function evaluations (NFE) for real-time control. The 3-Stage Safety Gate enables selective execution by detecting semantic OOD prompts using a Mahalanobis score in text-embedding space, filtering unstable generations via a directional sensitivity discrepancy metric, and enforcing final hard kinematic constraints such as joint and velocity limits before passing the generated trajectory to a low-level motion tracking controller. Extensive experiments on the Unitree G1 demonstrate that SafeFlow outperforms prior diffusion-based methods in success rate, physical compliance, and inference speed, while maintaining diverse expressiveness.

</details>


### [105] [Bio-Inspired Event-Based Visual Servoing for Ground Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23672)
*Maral Mordad,Kian Behzad,Debojyoti Biswas,Noah J. Cowan,Milad Siami*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于动态视觉传感器的事件驱动视觉伺服框架，通过空间核函数从事件流中直接提取运动状态，无需传统状态估计，并引入生物启发的主动感知极限环控制器解决平衡点线性可观测性丢失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受生物感知系统自适应性和主动感知行为启发，旨在开发计算和代谢效率高的机器人感知控制框架，利用事件相机的异步特性实现极低延迟的状态提取。

Method: 使用动态视觉传感器捕捉对数强度变化模式产生的事件流，应用固定空间核函数分析提取运动状态；线性空间剖面提取速度，二次空间剖面提取位置-速度乘积；采用多模式刺激直接合成非线性状态反馈；提出主动感知极限环控制器解决平衡点可观测性问题。

Result: 实验验证了所提直接感知方法的有效性、极低延迟性和计算效率；建立了事件率估计器的广义理论界限；在1/10比例自主地面车辆上成功实现了基于事件流的直接状态反馈控制。

Conclusion: 该事件驱动视觉伺服框架通过生物启发的主动感知机制，实现了无需传统状态估计的直接运动状态提取，为高效、低延迟的机器人控制提供了新途径。

Abstract: Biological sensory systems are inherently adaptive, filtering out constant stimuli and prioritizing relative changes, likely enhancing computational and metabolic efficiency. Inspired by active sensing behaviors across a wide range of animals, this paper presents a novel event-based visual servoing framework for ground robots. Utilizing a Dynamic Vision Sensor (DVS), we demonstrate that by applying a fixed spatial kernel to the asynchronous event stream generated from structured logarithmic intensity-change patterns, the resulting net event flux analytically isolates specific kinematic states. We establish a generalized theoretical bound for this event rate estimator and show that linear and quadratic spatial profiles isolate the robot's velocity and position-velocity product, respectively. Leveraging these properties, we employ a multi-pattern stimulus to directly synthesize a nonlinear state-feedback term entirely without traditional state estimation. To overcome the inescapable loss of linear observability at equilibrium inherent in event sensing, we propose a bio-inspired active sensing limit-cycle controller. Experimental validation on a 1/10-scale autonomous ground vehicle confirms the efficacy, extreme low-latency, and computational efficiency of the proposed direct-sensing approach.

</details>


### [106] [Learning What Can Be Picked: Active Reachability Estimation for Efficient Robotic Fruit Harvesting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23679)
*Nur Afsa Syeda,Mohamed Elmahallawy,Luis Fernando de la Torre,John Miller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出结合RGB-D感知与主动学习的框架，直接学习果实可达性作为二元决策问题，显著减少标注需求并提高预测精度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业劳动力短缺问题日益严重，机器人采摘系统在非结构化果园环境中部署受限，现有方法依赖耗时的逆运动学或运动规划计算果实可达性，导致计算效率低下和决策延迟

Method: 结合RGB-D感知与主动学习，将果实可达性建模为二元决策问题，使用主动学习策略（熵采样、边界采样等）选择信息量最大的样本进行标注，减少标注工作量

Result: 框架在显著减少标注样本的情况下实现准确的可达性预测，比随机采样高6-8%的准确率；在低标注量情况下，熵采样和边界采样优于委员会查询和标准不确定性采样；所有策略在标注集增大时性能趋于一致

Conclusion: 主动学习在农业机器人任务级感知中有效，为计算密集的运动学可达性分析提供了可扩展的替代方案，支持高效适应新果园配置

Abstract: Agriculture remains a cornerstone of global health and economic sustainability, yet labor-intensive tasks such as harvesting high-value crops continue to face growing workforce shortages. Robotic harvesting systems offer a promising solution; however, their deployment in unstructured orchard environments is constrained by inefficient perception-to-action pipelines. In particular, existing approaches often rely on exhaustive inverse kinematics or motion planning to determine whether a target fruit is reachable, leading to unnecessary computation and delayed decision-making. Our approach combines RGB-D perception with active learning to directly learn reachability as a binary decision problem. We then leverage active learning to selectively query the most informative samples for reachability labeling, significantly reducing annotation effort while maintaining high predictive accuracy. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves accurate reachability prediction with substantially fewer labeled samples, yielding approximately 6--8% higher accuracy than random sampling and enabling label-efficient adaptation to new orchard configurations. Among the evaluated strategies, entropy- and margin-based sampling outperform Query-by-Committee and standard uncertainty sampling in low-label regimes, while all strategies converge to comparable performance as the labeled set grows. These results highlight the effectiveness of active learning for task-level perception in agricultural robotics and position our approach as a scalable alternative to computation-heavy kinematic reachability analysis. Our code is available through https://github.com/wsu-cyber-security-lab-ai/active-learning.

</details>


### [107] [Equivariant Filter Transformations for Consistent and Efficient Visual--Inertial Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24130)
*Chungeng Tian,Fenghua He,Ning Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种等变滤波器变换方法，通过建立不同对称性EqF之间的解析联系，实现系统化一致性设计和高效实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-惯性导航中需要设计一致性的等变滤波器，但传统方法依赖特定对称性分析，缺乏系统性设计原则，且实现时计算负担较重。

Method: 1) 形式化全局系统状态到局部误差状态的映射，证明其诱导任意两个EqF误差状态间的非奇异线性变换；2) 推导关联线性化误差状态系统和非可观子空间的变换规律；3) 提出两种高效实现策略，利用具有块对角结构的简单EqF雅可比矩阵加速协方差运算。

Result: 蒙特卡洛仿真和真实世界实验验证了该方法在精度和运行时间方面的优越性，实现了系统性一致性设计和计算效率提升。

Conclusion: 该EqF变换方法为视觉-惯性导航提供了一种通用的系统性一致性设计框架，避免了特定对称性分析，并通过高效实现策略解决了计算负担问题。

Abstract: This paper presents an equivariant filter (EqF) transformation approach for visual--inertial navigation. By establishing analytical links between EqFs with different symmetries, the proposed approach enables systematic consistency design and efficient implementation. First, we formalize the mapping from the global system state to the local error-state and prove that it induces a nonsingular linear transformation between the error-states of any two EqFs. Second, we derive transformation laws for the associated linearized error-state systems and unobservable subspaces. These results yield a general consistency design principle: for any unobservable system, a consistent EqF with a state-independent unobservable subspace can be synthesized by transforming the local coordinate chart, thereby avoiding ad hoc symmetry analysis. Third, to mitigate the computational burden arising from the non-block-diagonal Jacobians required for consistency, we propose two efficient implementation strategies. These strategies exploit the Jacobians of a simpler EqF with block-diagonal structure to accelerate covariance operations while preserving consistency. Extensive Monte Carlo simulations and real-world experiments validate the proposed approach in terms of both accuracy and runtime.

</details>


### [108] [Form-Fitting, Large-Area Sensor Mounting for Obstacle Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23725)
*Anna Soukhovei,Carson Kohlbrenner,Caleb Escobedo,Alexander Gholmieh,Alexander Dickhans,Alessandro Roncone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种低成本方法，将传感器安装到机器人连杆上实现大面积传感覆盖，无需在使用前校准传感器位置和方向


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在机器人非可展曲面（无法无失真展平为2D平面的3D表面）上安装传感器时，需要预先校准传感器位置和方向的问题，降低部署成本

Method: 使用CAD程序化生成机器人皮肤覆盖单元，可适配非可展曲面；皮肤单元嵌入任意尺寸PCB的安装座，将传感器固定在已知位置；通过安装在打印皮肤单元上的ToF成像器阵列构建点云图像

Result: 成功在Franka Research 3机器人手臂上部署打印皮肤单元，使用ToF成像器阵列构建了机器人操作环境中障碍物的点云图像

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种低成本、无需预校准的传感器部署方案，可实现机器人连杆的大面积传感覆盖，适用于非可展曲面

Abstract: We introduce a low-cost method for mounting sensors onto robot links for large-area sensing coverage that does not require the sensor's positions or orientations to be calibrated before use. Using computer aided design (CAD), a robot skin covering, or skin unit, can be procedurally generated to fit around a nondevelopable surface, a 3D surface that cannot be flattened into a 2D plane without distortion, of a robot. The skin unit embeds mounts for printed circuit boards of any size to keep sensors in fixed and known locations. We demonstrate our method by constructing point cloud images of obstacles within the proximity of a Franka Research 3 robot's operational environment using an array of time of flight (ToF) imagers mounted on a printed skin unit and attached to the robot arm.

</details>


### [109] [Task-Space Singularity Avoidance for Control Affine Systems Using Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23753)
*Kimia Forghani,Suraj Raval,Lamar Mair,Axel Krieger,Yancy Diaz-Mercado*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于控制屏障函数(CBF)的框架，用于避免控制仿射系统中的奇异性，通过特征值检测奇异配置并构建屏障函数保持安全边界，在机械臂和磁控针系统中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人和动力系统中的奇异性会导致控制输入到任务空间运动的映射降秩，无法确定输入，限制系统在期望方向生成力和扭矩的能力，并阻碍精确轨迹跟踪。现有方法在避免奇异性方面存在不足。

Method: 基于控制屏障函数(CBF)框架，通过状态相关的输入-输出映射矩阵的特征值识别奇异配置，构建屏障函数以保持与降秩区域的安全边界。提供了考虑执行器动力学的理论安全保证条件。

Result: 在平面2连杆机械臂和磁控针系统上的仿真表明，该方法能实现平滑轨迹跟踪同时避免奇异配置，与标称控制器相比，控制输入峰值降低了高达100倍。

Conclusion: 提出的CBF框架能有效避免控制仿射系统中的奇异性，提供理论安全保证，显著改善轨迹跟踪性能并大幅降低控制输入峰值，为机器人系统避免奇异配置提供了系统化方法。

Abstract: Singularities in robotic and dynamical systems arise when the mapping from control inputs to task-space motion loses rank, leading to an inability to determine inputs. This limits the system's ability to generate forces and torques in desired directions and prevents accurate trajectory tracking. This paper presents a control barrier function (CBF) framework for avoiding such singularities in control-affine systems. Singular configurations are identified through the eigenvalues of a state-dependent input-output mapping matrix, and barrier functions are constructed to maintain a safety margin from rank-deficient regions. Conditions for theoretical guarantees on safety are provided as a function of actuator dynamics. Simulations on a planar 2-link manipulator and a magnetically actuated needle demonstrate smooth trajectory tracking while avoiding singular configurations and reducing control input spikes by up to 100x compared to the nominal controller.

</details>


### [110] [Object Search in Partially-Known Environments via LLM-informed Model-based Planning and Prompt Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23800)
*Abhishek Paudel,Abhish Khanal,Raihan I. Arnob,Shahriar Hossain,Gregory J. Stein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于LLM的模型规划框架和提示选择方法，用于部分已知环境中的目标物体搜索，通过LLM估计位置搜索概率并结合环境地图的移动成本进行规划，同时利用离线重放实现快速提示和LLM选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分已知环境中进行目标物体搜索时，需要有效结合LLM的语义理解能力和传统规划方法，同时解决如何快速选择最优提示和LLM的问题，以提高搜索效率和降低搜索成本。

Method: 1) LLM-informed模型规划框架：使用LLM估计在不同位置找到目标物体的概率，结合环境地图提取的移动成本，构建搜索模型进行规划；2) 基于离线重放的提示选择方法：利用最近提出的离线重放方法，在部署时快速选择最佳提示和LLM，采用类似bandit的快速选择策略。

Result: 仿真实验显示：1) LLM-informed规划方法比完全依赖LLM的基线规划策略提升11.8%，比乐观策略提升39.2%；2) bandit-like选择方法比基线UCB bandit选择降低6.5%的平均成本和33.8%的平均累积遗憾。真实机器人实验在公寓环境中验证了类似改进。

Conclusion: 提出的LLM-informed模型规划框架有效结合了LLM的语义理解和传统规划方法，在部分已知环境物体搜索任务中显著优于基线方法；同时，基于离线重放的提示选择方法能够快速选择最优提示和LLM，进一步提高了系统性能。

Abstract: We present a novel LLM-informed model-based planning framework, and a novel prompt selection method, for object search in partially-known environments. Our approach uses an LLM to estimate statistics about the likelihood of finding the target object when searching various locations throughout the scene that, combined with travel costs extracted from the environment map, are used to instantiate a model, thus using the LLM to inform planning and achieve effective search performance. Moreover, the abstraction upon which our approach relies is amenable to deployment-time model selection via the recent offline replay approach, an insight we leverage to enable fast prompt and LLM selection during deployment. Simulation experiments demonstrate that our LLM-informed model-based planning approach outperforms the baseline planning strategy that fully relies on LLM and optimistic strategy with as much as 11.8% and 39.2% improvements respectively, and our bandit-like selection approach enables quick selection of best prompts and LLMs resulting in 6.5% lower average cost and 33.8% lower average cumulative regret over baseline UCB bandit selection. Real-robot experiments in an apartment demonstrate similar improvements and so further validate our approach.

</details>


### [111] [AgentChemist: A Multi-Agent Experimental Robotic Platform Integrating Chemical Perception and Precise Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23886)
*Xiangyi Wei,Fei Wang,Haotian Zhang,Xin An,Haitian Zhu,Lianrui Hu,Yang Li,Changbo Wang,Xiao He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 多智能体机器人平台通过协作任务分解、动态调度和自适应控制解决化学实验室自动化的长尾分布问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学实验室自动化长期受限于僵化的工作流程和对实验任务长尾分布的适应性差。现有自动化平台在标准化程序上表现良好，但无法处理真实实验室中多样化、低频次和不断演变的操作，这些操作超出了预定义协议的范围，导致系统难以适应新的反应条件、罕见仪器配置和意外程序变化。

Method: 提出一个多智能体机器人平台，采用协作任务分解、动态调度和自适应控制策略。系统集成了化学感知功能用于实时反应监测，并结合反馈驱动的执行机制，使其能够根据实验状态的变化调整操作，而不是依赖固定脚本。

Result: 通过酸碱滴定实验验证，系统展示了自主进度跟踪、自适应分配控制和可靠的端到端实验执行能力。

Conclusion: 该平台通过提高对多样化实验室场景的泛化能力，为实现智能、灵活和可扩展的实验室自动化提供了实用途径。

Abstract: Chemical laboratory automation has long been constrained by rigid workflows and poor adaptability to the long-tail distribution of experimental tasks. While most automated platforms perform well on a narrow set of standardized procedures, real laboratories involve diverse, infrequent, and evolving operations that fall outside predefined protocols. This mismatch prevents existing systems from generalizing to novel reaction conditions, uncommon instrument configurations, and unexpected procedural variations. We present a multi-agent robotic platform designed to address this long-tail challenge through collaborative task decomposition, dynamic scheduling, and adaptive control. The system integrates chemical perception for real-time reaction monitoring with feedback-driven execution, enabling it to adjust actions based on evolving experimental states rather than fixed scripts. Validation via acid-base titration demonstrates autonomous progress tracking, adaptive dispensing control, and reliable end-to-end experiment execution. By improving generalization across diverse laboratory scenarios, this platform provides a practical pathway toward intelligent, flexible, and scalable laboratory automation.

</details>


### [112] [Event-Driven Proactive Assistive Manipulation with Grounded Vision-Language Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23950)
*Fengkai Liu,Hao Su,Haozhuang Chi,Rui Geng,Congzhi Ren,Xuqing Liu,Yucheng Xu,Yuichi Ohsita,Liyun Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出从请求驱动转向事件驱动的主动协助框架，通过监控工作空间状态转换来推断任务目标并生成协助动作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在流畅的人类团队协作中，伙伴通常通过观察动作结果来推断下一步帮助，而非等待指令。现有机器人协助多为请求驱动，需要用户明确指令，这限制了协作的自然性和效率。

Method: 提出事件驱动框架：1) 事件监控器跟踪人-物交互进度；2) 事件完成后提取稳定的前后状态快照；3) 规划器分析状态转换推断任务级目标，决定是否干预；4) 若干预则生成基于动作原语和整数ID引用对象的可执行动作序列。

Result: 在真实桌面数字积木协作任务上评估，证明显式的前后状态变化证据能提高可解场景的主动完成率和不可解场景的适当等待。

Conclusion: 事件驱动的主动协助框架通过监控状态转换而非依赖用户指令，能更自然地推断协作需求，提高人机协作的流畅性和效率。

Abstract: Assistance in collaborative manipulation is often initiated by user instructions, making high-level reasoning request-driven. In fluent human teamwork, however, partners often infer the next helpful step from the observed outcome of an action rather than waiting for instructions. Motivated by this, we introduce a shift from request-driven assistance to event-driven proactive assistance, where robot actions are initiated by workspace state transitions induced by human--object interactions rather than user-provided task instructions. To this end, we propose an event-driven framework that tracks interaction progress with an event monitor and, upon event completion, extracts stabilized pre/post snapshots that characterize the resulting state transition. Given the stabilized snapshots, the planner analyzes the implied state transition to infer a task-level goal and decide whether to intervene; if so, it generates a sequence of assistive actions. To make outputs executable and verifiable, we restrict actions to a set of action primitives and reference objects via integer IDs. We evaluate the framework on a real tabletop number-block collaboration task, demonstrating that explicit pre/post state-change evidence improves proactive completion on solvable scenes and appropriate waiting on unsolvable ones.

</details>


### [113] [MonoSIM: An open source SIL framework for Ackermann Vehicular Systems with Monocular Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23965)
*Shantanu Rahman,Nayeb Hasin,Mainul Islam,Md. Zubair Alom Rony,Golam Sarowar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开源SIL仿真平台，用于阿克曼转向自动驾驶车辆的研究与教育，支持单目视觉和低成本实验平台，验证了MPC和PID控制算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为自主阿克曼车辆研究和教育提供一个简单、易用的开源仿真平台，支持小规模实验设置（如XTENTH-CAR平台），降低算法验证成本。

Method: 使用开源工具构建软件在环仿真平台，集成单目视觉系统，采用滑动窗口车道线检测方法降低计算开销，支持灵活算法测试环境。

Result: 平台成功验证了MPC和PID控制算法，证明其能提供可靠的算法验证环境，适用于多智能体系统研究、教育和低成本AGV开发。

Conclusion: 该SIL仿真平台是自主车辆算法开发的有效工具，具有开源、低成本、易用等优势，适合研究和教育应用。

Abstract: This paper presents an open-source Software-in-the-Loop (SIL) simulation platform designed for autonomous Ackerman vehicle research and education. The proposed framework focuses on simplicity, while making it easy to work with small-scale experimental setups, such as the XTENTH-CAR platform. The system was designed using open source tools, creating an environment with a monocular camera vision system to capture stimuli from it with minimal computational overhead through a sliding window based lane detection method. The platform supports a flexible algorithm testing and validation environment, allowing researchers to implement and compare various control strategies within an easy-to-use virtual environment. To validate the working of the platform, Model Predictive Control (MPC) and Proportional-Integral-Derivative (PID) algorithms were implemented within the SIL framework. The results confirm that the platform provides a reliable environment for algorithm verification, making it an ideal tool for future multi-agent system research, educational purposes, and low-cost AGV development. Our code is available at https://github.com/shantanu404/monosim.git.

</details>


### [114] [Robust Distributed Cooperative Path-Following and Local Replanning for Multi-UAVs Under Differentiated Low-Altitude Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23968)
*Zimao Sheng,Zirui Yu,Hong'an Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种多固定翼无人机在复杂数字高程模型低空空域下的协同路径跟踪控制策略，解决了风场扰动、突发障碍和分布式时间同步等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法缺乏高效的分布式协调机制来实现三维差异化路径的时间一致性跟踪，无法量化抗扰动鲁棒性，且缺乏有效的在线避障重规划能力。

Method: 1) 通过时间指标量化分布式协同路径跟踪问题，采用分布式通信协议确保一致性；2) 开发纵向-横向前视角调整方法与鲁棒制导律相结合，实现风扰动下路径跟踪误差的有限时间稳定归零；3) 设计具有最小时间成本的高效局部路径重规划方法，用于实时在线避障。

Result: 实验验证证明了所提策略的有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: 该策略成功解决了多固定翼无人机在复杂DEM低空空域下的协同路径跟踪问题，实现了抗扰动鲁棒性、时间同步和实时避障重规划的综合性能提升。

Abstract: Multiple fixed-wing unmanned aerial vehicles (multi-UAVs) encounter significant challenges in cooperative path following over complex Digital Elevation Model (DEM) low-altitude airspace, including wind field disturbances, sudden obstacles, and requirements of distributed temporal synchronization during differentiated path tracking. Existing methods lack efficient distributed coordination mechanisms for time-consistent tracking of 3D differentiated paths, fail to quantify robustness against disturbances, and lack effective online obstacle avoidance replanning capabilities. To address these gaps, a cooperative control strategy is proposed: first, the distributed cooperative path-following problem is quantified via time indices, and consistency is ensured through a distributed communication protocol; second, a longitudinal-lateral look-ahead angle adjustment method coupled with a robust guidance law is developed to achieve finite-time stabilization of path following error to zero under wind disturbances; third, an efficient local path replanning method with minimal time cost is designed for real-time online obstacle avoidance.Experimental validations demonstrate the effectiveness and superiority of the $\ $proposed strategy.

</details>


### [115] [MIRROR: Visual Motion Imitation via Real-time Retargeting and Teleoperation with Parallel Differential Inverse Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23995)
*Junheng Li,Lizhi Yang,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GPU并行化、基于延拓的微分逆运动学方法，用于实时人形机器人遥操作，通过并行求解多个约束IK二次规划并利用控制屏障函数避免自碰撞，提高从约束诱导局部极小值的逃逸能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时人形机器人遥操作需要既响应迅速又满足约束安全的逆运动学求解器。传统微分IK的局部线性化更新存在盆地依赖性，容易在关节极限、奇点或碰撞边界附近陷入局部极小值，导致不安全或停滞行为。

Method: 提出GPU并行化、基于延拓的微分IK方法：1）并行评估多个约束IK二次规划；2）结合自碰撞避免控制屏障函数；3）基于Lyapunov的进展准则选择能减少最终全局任务空间误差的更新；4）与视觉骨骼姿态估计管道配对，实现实时上体遥操作。

Result: 该方法在THEMIS人形机器人硬件上实现了鲁棒的实时上体遥操作，在真实世界任务中表现出色，提高了从约束诱导局部极小值的逃逸能力，同时保持了实时性能。

Conclusion: 提出的GPU并行化、基于延拓的微分IK方法能够有效改善实时人形机器人遥操作中的约束安全性、稳定性和响应性，通过并行求解和进展准则的结合，解决了传统微分IK在约束条件下的局部极小值问题。

Abstract: Real-time humanoid teleoperation requires inverse kinematics (IK) solvers that are both responsive and constraint-safe under kinematic redundancy and self-collision constraints. While differential IK enables efficient online retargeting, its locally linearized updates are inherently basin-dependent and often become trapped near joint limits, singularities, or active collision boundaries, leading to unsafe or stagnant behavior. We propose a GPU-parallelized, continuation-based differential IK that improves escape from such constraint-induced local minima while preserving real-time performance, promoting safety and stability. Multiple constrained IK quadratic programs are evaluated in parallel, together with a self-collision avoidance control barrier function (CBF), and a Lyapunov-based progression criterion selects updates that reduce the final global task-space error. The method is paired with a visual skeletal pose estimation pipeline that enables robust, real-time upper-body teleoperation on the THEMIS humanoid robot hardware in real-world tasks.

</details>


### [116] [QuadFM: Foundational Text-Driven Quadruped Motion Dataset for Generation and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24021)
*Li Gao,Fuzhi Yang,Jianhui Chen,Liu Liu,Yao Zheng,Yang Cai,Ziqiao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: QuadFM是首个大规模、高保真的四足机器人基础运动数据集，包含11,784个运动片段和35,352个语言描述，支持文本到运动生成和通用运动控制，并提出了Gen2Control RL统一框架实现实时运动合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前四足机器人运动数据集局限于少数基本动作（如行走、小跑、坐下），缺乏多样化的行为表达和丰富的语言语义基础，这限制了敏捷、直观的人机交互能力的发展。

Method: 1) 构建QuadFM数据集：包含11,784个精选运动片段，涵盖运动、交互和情感表达行为，每个片段有三层标注（细粒度动作标签、交互场景、自然语言命令）；2) 提出Gen2Control RL框架：联合训练通用运动控制器和文本到运动生成器，实现端到端推理。

Result: 在配备NVIDIA Orin的真实四足机器人上，系统实现了实时运动合成（延迟<500毫秒）。仿真和真实世界实验展示了逼真、多样的运动，同时保持了稳健的物理交互能力。

Conclusion: QuadFM填补了四足机器人基础运动资源的空白，通过结合多样化运动、情感表达行为和丰富语言语义，为敏捷、直观的人机交互提供了重要基础。数据集将开源发布。

Abstract: Despite significant advances in quadrupedal robotics, a critical gap persists in foundational motion resources that holistically integrate diverse locomotion, emotionally expressive behaviors, and rich language semantics-essential for agile, intuitive human-robot interaction. Current quadruped motion datasets are limited to a few mocap primitives (e.g., walk, trot, sit) and lack diverse behaviors with rich language grounding. To bridge this gap, we introduce Quadruped Foundational Motion (QuadFM) , the first large-scale, ultra-high-fidelity dataset designed for text-to-motion generation and general motion control. QuadFM contains 11,784 curated motion clips spanning locomotion, interactive, and emotion-expressive behaviors (e.g., dancing, stretching, peeing), each with three-layer annotation-fine-grained action labels, interaction scenarios, and natural language commands-totaling 35,352 descriptions to support language-conditioned understanding and command execution.
  We further propose Gen2Control RL, a unified framework that jointly trains a general motion controller and a text-to-motion generator, enabling efficient end-to-end inference on edge hardware. On a real quadruped robot with an NVIDIA Orin, our system achieves real-time motion synthesis (<500 ms latency). Simulation and real-world results show realistic, diverse motions while maintaining robust physical interaction. The dataset will be released at https://github.com/GaoLii/QuadFM.

</details>


### [117] [PCHC: Enabling Preference Conditioned Humanoid Control via Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24047)
*Huanyu Li,Dewei Wang,Xinmiao Wang,Xinzhe Liu,Peng Liu,Chenjia Bai,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于多目标强化学习的偏好条件人形机器人控制框架，通过单个策略实现多样化行为，无需训练多个策略来近似帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前强化学习方法在人形机器人控制中采用固定权重策略，只能产生单一子优策略，无法提供多样化的多目标控制解决方案。需要一种能够根据偏好条件实时调整目标优先级的框架。

Method: 提出偏好条件人形机器人控制框架，采用多目标强化学习。引入基于Beta分布的偏好向量对齐机制，调制混合专家模块，使单个策略能够展现广泛的行为谱。

Result: 在两个代表性人形机器人任务上验证，通过大量仿真和真实世界实验证明，该框架使机器人能够根据输入偏好条件实时自适应调整目标优先级。

Conclusion: 提出的多目标强化学习框架成功实现了偏好条件人形机器人控制，单个策略即可提供多样化行为，优于需要训练多个策略的传统方法。

Abstract: Humanoid robots often need to balance competing objectives, such as maximizing speed while minimizing energy consumption.
